# People of the Fitna — Profiles II: ⁨الكوفة⁩, and the men of ⁨صفين⁩

**Question this note answers.** Session 3 («⁨پہلا امتحان⁩», 23–40 AH) cannot be told through the four
caliphs alone. Between the killing of ⁨عثمان ؓ⁩ and the killing of ⁨علي ؓ⁩ the decisive actors are a
second rank of men — governors, commanders, tribal chiefs and arbitrators — whom the room has never
heard of and who are, in the sources, mostly *Companions*. This note builds the page-cited ⁨تراجم⁩
register for twelve of them, so that each can be introduced **inside the story at the moment he
becomes load-bearing**, in one breath, with his clan, his dates and one sentence of what he did
(`DECISIONS.md` #28). It also collects, verbatim, every place where **⁨الذهبي⁩ himself refuses,
grades, doubts or declines to adjudicate** a report about these men — because that, and not our own
phrasing, is what lets the speaker decline to adjudicate from the platform.

---

Every page in the ranges above was read in full. Most were already cached under
`sources/shamela/10906/<index>.txt`; **nine pages were fetched for this note** with
`python tools/shamela.py`, because the ⁨ترجمه⁩ of ⁨قيس بن سعد ؓ⁩ and of ⁨جرير ؓ⁩ continued past the end of
the cached block: **2717–2721** and **2516–2518**. They are now cached like the rest, and every one
of them gets its line in `docs/catalogue/SHAMELA_LOG.md`.

**Status.** Written 2026-09-12 by the session-3 research pass. Scope: the ⁨تراجم⁩ register for the men
of 23–41 AH other than the caliphs themselves; it does **not** narrate ⁨الجمل⁩ or ⁨صفين⁩ as events —
those belong to the battle-narrative notes — and it does **not** re-derive anything from the
11–23 AH window, which is already complete and committed.

## ⚠ Standing warning about the Arabic on this page

Every Arabic blockquote below was **extracted mechanically** from the cached page file named beneath
it — copied, never retyped and never composed. The printed edition's own footnote markers
**(١) (٢) (٣)** are reproduced where they fall inside a quoted sentence; they are the Rasāla
editor's markers, not part of the classical text, and they must be **deleted before the line reaches
a slide**. Round-bracket material inside a quotation is likewise the printed edition's own
parenthesis (⁨الذهبي⁩'s edition encloses prophetic speech in round brackets).

**Nothing here goes on a projector until the page image has been eyeballed** — `CLAUDE.md` §1.1.
The text layer is reliable for locating and page-anchoring; a single garbled letter changes the word.

Printed pages are cited as the file's own `PRINTED PAGE:` header gives them, in Arabic-Indic digits,
**never** the Shamela index. ⁨سیر⁩ pages are cited `⁨سیر ج۳ ص۵۴⁩`.

---

## 0. The sentences this note licenses

Every claim below may go on a slide, a cue card or a script **as written**. Anything not in this list
is not yet licensed by this note. Read this section instead of the whole note.

**عمرو بن العاص ؓ**
- ⁨الذهبي⁩ calls him **⁨داهية قريش⁩** — "the shrewd one of Quraysh … and the man of the age." `[SOURCED]` ⁨سیر ج۳ ص۵۵⁩
- He became Muslim **early in 8 AH**, travelling with **⁨خالد بن الوليد ؓ⁩** and **⁨عثمان بن طلحة ؓ⁩**. `[SOURCED]` ⁨سیر ج۳ ص۵۵⁩
- The Prophet ﷺ set him over the army of **⁨ذات السلاسل⁩**, and **⁨أبو بكر ؓ⁩ and ⁨عمر ؓ⁩ were in it**. `[SOURCED]` ⁨سیر ج۳ ص۶۷⁩ (⁨البخاري⁩, ⁨مسلم⁩)
- He was **older than ⁨عمر ؓ⁩** and remembered the night ⁨عمر⁩ was born. `[SOURCED]` ⁨سیر ج۳ ص۵۸⁩
- **Alexandria fell in 21 AH** and revolted in **25**; **⁨طرابلس الغرب⁩ in 24**, and it is said **23**. `[SOURCED]` ⁨سیر ج۳ ص۷۰⁩
- **⁨عثمان ؓ⁩ removed him from Egypt and appointed ⁨عبد الله بن أبي سرح⁩.** `[SOURCED]` ⁨سیر ج۳ ص۷۱⁩
- He stayed in **Mecca until the day of ⁨الجمل⁩**, then went to ⁨معاوية ؓ⁩, who gave him Egypt; he entered it as governor **in 39 AH** and died about three years later. `[SOURCED]` ⁨سیر ج۳ ص۷۱⁩–۷۳
- He died **on the night of ʿĪd al-Fiṭr**, **43 AH** by most reports, **42** by others; ⁨الذهبي⁩ calls the report of **51** an error in his own words. `[SOURCED]` ⁨سیر ج۳ ص۷۷⁩
- ⁨الذهبي⁩'s summing-up, quotable in full, ending `⁨فَإِنَّ لَهُ سَابِقَةً لَيْسَتْ لِمُعَاوِيَةَ⁩`. `[SOURCED]` ⁨سیر ج۳ ص۵۹⁩
- The Alexandria dialogue and the Byzantine's warning. `[SOURCED]` ⁨سیر ج۳ ص۷۰⁩–۷۱

**أبو موسى الأشعري ؓ**
- **⁨عبد الله بن قيس⁩**; ⁨الذهبي⁩: `⁨الإِمَامُ الكَبِيْرُ⁩ ... ⁨الفَقِيْهُ، المُقْرِئُ⁩`. `[SOURCED]` ⁨سیر ج۲ ص۳۸۰⁩–۳۸۱
- He reached Medina **in the days of ⁨خيبر⁩**; the Prophet ﷺ told the Ashʿarīs `⁨لَكُمُ الهِجْرَةُ مَرَّتَيْنِ⁩`. `[SOURCED]` ⁨سیر ج۲ ص۳۸۲⁩ · ⁨ج۲ ص۳۸۴⁩
- **⁨مسروق⁩ counts him one of the six of judgement** among the Companions. `[SOURCED]` ⁨سیر ج۲ ص۳۸۸⁩
- He governed **Basra from 17 AH**, took **⁨الأهواز⁩**, **⁨تستر⁩** and **⁨أصبهان⁩ in 23 AH**. `[SOURCED]` ⁨سیر ج۲ ص۳۹۰⁩–۳۹۱
- **⁨عمر ؓ⁩'s will: no governor more than a year — and keep the Ashʿarī four.** `[SOURCED]` ⁨سیر ج۲ ص۳۹۱⁩
- **⁨عثمان ؓ⁩ removed him from Basra** and appointed ⁨عبد الله بن عامر⁩; he afterwards held **Kufa** and died there. `[SOURCED]` ⁨سیر ج۲ ص۳۸۲⁩ · ⁨ج۲ ص۳۹۰⁩
- He left Basra with **six hundred dirhams**, his family's stipend. `[SOURCED]` ⁨سیر ج۲ ص۳۹۱⁩ · ⁨ج۲ ص۳۹۸⁩
- **⁨معاوية ؓ⁩'s written offer and his four-line refusal**, chain sound. `[SOURCED]` ⁨سیر ج۲ ص۳۹۶⁩
- ⁨الذهبي⁩ on ⁨التحكيم⁩: he deposed ⁨علي ؓ⁩, deposed ⁨معاوية ؓ⁩, proposed ⁨ابن عمر ؓ⁩ — `⁨فَمَا انْتَظَمَ مِنْ ذَلِكَ حَالٌ⁩`. `[SOURCED]` ⁨سیر ج۲ ص۳۹۴⁩
- ⁨الذهبي⁩'s verdict on the man: `⁨صَوَّاماً، قَوَّاماً، رَبَّانِيّاً⁩ ... ⁨لَمْ تُغَيِّرْهُ الإِمَارَةُ⁩`. `[SOURCED]` ⁨سیر ج۲ ص۳۹۶⁩
- **Death: ⁨ذو الحجة⁩ 44 AH on ⁨الذهبي⁩'s own preference**, with 42, 43, 52 and 53 also recorded. `[SOURCED]` ⁨سیر ج۲ ص۳۹۷⁩–۳۹۸

**المغيرة بن شعبة ؓ**
- `⁨مِنْ كِبَارِ الصَّحَابَةِ أُوْلِي الشَّجَاعَةِ وَالمَكِيْدَةِ⁩`; present at **⁨بيعة الرضوان⁩**. `[SOURCED]` ⁨سیر ج۳ ص۲۱⁩
- Of **⁨ثقيف⁩**, of **⁨الأحلاف⁩**; nephew of **⁨عروة بن مسعود ؓ⁩**. `[SOURCED]` ⁨سیر ج۳ ص۲۴⁩–۲۵
- He killed thirteen of **⁨بنو مالك⁩** before Islam; the Prophet ﷺ accepted his Islam and refused the spoils: `⁨لأَنَّ هَذَا غَدْرٌ، وَلاَ خَيْرَ فِي الغَدْرِ⁩`. `[SOURCED]` ⁨سیر ج۳ ص۲۵⁩
- He was **the last man out of the Prophet's ﷺ grave**. `[SOURCED]` ⁨سیر ج۳ ص۲۶⁩
- Governor of **⁨البحرين⁩**, then **⁨البصرة⁩ for three years**, then **⁨الكوفة⁩**. `[SOURCED]` ⁨سیر ج۳ ص۲۶⁩–۲۸
- **⁨أذربيجان⁩ fell in 22 AH under his command**; ⁨همذان⁩ by force. `[SOURCED]` ⁨سیر ج۳ ص۲۸⁩
- He was **the first man greeted with the formula of command from the pulpit**. `[SOURCED]` ⁨سیر ج۳ ص۲۸⁩
- **⁨الزهري⁩'s five ⁨دهاة⁩ of the fitna**, and where each stood. `[SOURCED]` ⁨سیر ج۳ ص۲۲⁩–۲۳ · ⁨ج۳ ص۱۰۸⁩
- He **withdrew from both sides** and **led the pilgrimage in the year 40**. `[SOURCED]` ⁨سیر ج۳ ص۲۹⁩
- **⁨عمرو ؓ⁩'s advice to ⁨معاوية ؓ⁩**: use his judgement, and keep him away from the treasury. `[SOURCED]` ⁨سیر ج۳ ص۳۰⁩
- **Death ⁨شعبان⁩ 50 AH, aged seventy, as governor of Kufa**; ⁨جرير ؓ⁩'s words over him. `[SOURCED]` ⁨سیر ج۳ ص۳۱⁩–۳۲
- The **⁨القادسية⁩ embassy**, and the eye put out the next day. `[SOURCED]` ⁨سیر ج۳ ص۳۲⁩
- **His eye: four accounts** — ⁨اليرموك⁩, ⁨القادسية⁩, ⁨الطائف⁩, the eclipse — and the sources choose none. `[SOURCED]` ⁨سیر ج۳ ص۲۱⁩ · ⁨ج۳ ص۲۳⁩–۲۴ · ⁨ج۳ ص۳۲⁩

**الأشتر مالك بن الحارث النخعي**
- **Not a Companion**; he narrated from **⁨عمر ؓ⁩** and **⁨خالد ؓ⁩**; he lost an eye at **⁨اليرموك⁩**. `[SOURCED]` ⁨سیر ج۴ ص۳۴⁩
- ⁨الذهبي⁩: `⁨شَهْماً، مُطَاعاً، زَعِراً⁩ (١) ⁨، أَلَبَّ عَلَى عُثْمَانَ، وَقَاتَلَهُ⁩` — quotable **as ⁨الذهبي⁩'s words**. `[SOURCED]` ⁨سیر ج۴ ص۳۴⁩
- At **⁨صفين⁩** he nearly routed ⁨معاوية ؓ⁩ and **was stopped by ⁨علي ؓ⁩'s own men when the maṣāḥif went up**. `[SOURCED]` ⁨سیر ج۴ ص۳۴⁩
- **⁨عمر ؓ⁩**: `⁨إِنَّ لِلْمُسْلِمِيْنَ مِنْ هَذَا يَوْماً عَصِيْباً⁩`. `[SOURCED]` ⁨سیر ج۴ ص۳۴⁩
- He was sent to govern Egypt after ⁨صفين⁩ and **died poisoned on the road**; ⁨علي ؓ⁩'s lament. `[SOURCED]` ⁨سیر ج۴ ص۳۴⁩
- **The year of his death is not on the page.** `[CONVENTIONAL-ESTIMATE]` only.
- His son **⁨إبراهيم⁩** killed **⁨عبيد الله بن زياد⁩** at ⁨الخازر⁩ and was killed with ⁨مصعب⁩ in **72 AH**. `[SOURCED]` ⁨سیر ج۴ ص۳۵⁩

**محمد بن أبي بكر ؓ**
- **Born to ⁨أسماء بنت عميس ؓ⁩ during ⁨حجة الوداع⁩, at iḥrām.** `[SOURCED]` ⁨سیر ج۳ ص۴۸۲⁩
- **⁨عثمان ؓ⁩ had appointed him over Egypt**; ⁨الذهبي⁩ records that he was then among those who rose against him — quotable **only as ⁨الذهبي⁩'s sentence**. `[SOURCED]` ⁨سیر ج۳ ص۴۸۲⁩
- **⁨علي ؓ⁩ sent him to govern Egypt in ⁨رمضان⁩ 37 AH**; he was defeated, betrayed and killed there. `[SOURCED]` ⁨سیر ج۳ ص۴۸۲⁩
- `⁨احْفَظُونِي فِي أَبِي بَكْرٍ⁩` and ⁨معاوية بن حديج⁩'s answer. `[SOURCED]` ⁨سیر ج۳ ص۴۸۲⁩
- **Who killed him: two accounts on one page** — ⁨معاوية بن حديج⁩, and ⁨عمرو بن العاص ؓ⁩. `[SOURCED]` ⁨سیر ج۳ ص۴۸۲⁩
- His son is **⁨القاسم بن محمد⁩ the jurist**. `[SOURCED]` ⁨سیر ج۳ ص۴۸۲⁩
- ⁨الذهبي⁩: `⁨عَامَّةُ مَنْ سَعَى فِي دَمِ عُثْمَانَ قُتِلُوا، وَعَسَى القَتْلُ خَيْراً لَهُم وَتَمْحِيْصاً⁩`. `[SOURCED]` ⁨سیر ج۳ ص۴۸۱⁩

**هاشم بن عتبة**
- **⁨المِرقال⁩**, of **⁨بنو زهرة⁩**, **nephew of ⁨سعد بن أبي وقاص ؓ⁩**. `[SOURCED]` ⁨سیر ج۳ ص۴۸۶⁩
- **Born in the Prophet's ﷺ lifetime**; lost an eye at **⁨اليرموك⁩**; present at the **conquest of Damascus**. `[SOURCED]` ⁨سیر ج۳ ص۴۸۶⁩
- He **carried ⁨علي ؓ⁩'s standard at ⁨صفين⁩ and was killed that day**. `[SOURCED]` ⁨سیر ج۳ ص۴۸۶⁩
- **Some counted him a Companion because he lived in the Prophet's ﷺ time** — ⁨الذهبي⁩ says only that, and closes with ⁨رحمہ اللہ⁩. `[SOURCED]` ⁨سیر ج۳ ص۴۸۶⁩

**حجر بن عدي**
- **⁨حُجْر الخير⁩**, of **⁨كندة⁩**; `⁨لَهُ⁩: ⁨صُحْبَةٌ، وَوِفَادَةٌ⁩` and **no narration from the Prophet ﷺ**. `[SOURCED]` ⁨سیر ج۳ ص۴۶۳⁩
- He fought at **⁨القادسية⁩** and **opened ⁨مرج عذراء⁩**. `[SOURCED]` ⁨سیر ج۳ ص۴۶۳⁩
- ⁨الذهبي⁩: `⁨شَرِيفاً، أَمِيْراً مُطَاعاً، أَمَّاراً بِالمَعْرُوفِ، مُقْدِماً عَلَى الإِنْكَارِ⁩ ... ⁨وَكَانَ ذَا صَلاَحٍ وَتَعَبُّدٍ⁩`. `[SOURCED]` ⁨سیر ج۳ ص۴۶۳⁩
- **Killed with his companions at ⁨عذراء⁩ in 51 AH**; they were thirteen, **seven killed and six spared** when a release arrived. `[SOURCED]` ⁨سیر ج۳ ص۴۶۵⁩ · ⁨ج۳ ص۴۶۷⁩
- His words at ⁨عذراء⁩: the village he opened; the two long rakʿas; `⁨سَيْفاً مَشْهُوْراً، وَكَفَناً مَنْشُوراً، وَقَبْراً مَحْفُوراً⁩`; `⁨اللَّهُمَّ إِنَّا نَسْتَعْدِيكَ⁩`; `⁨إِنَّ ذَلِكَ لَدَمٌ مَا كُنْتُ لأُعِيْنَ عَلَيْهِ⁩`. `[SOURCED]` ⁨سیر ج۳ ص۴۶۴⁩–۴۶۶
- **⁨عائشہ ؓ⁩ sent to ask for their release** and the messenger arrived too late; **⁨معاوية ؓ⁩ regretted it**, in ⁨الذهبي⁩'s own gloss. `[SOURCED]` ⁨سیر ج۳ ص۴۶۴⁩–۴۶۵
- **⁨ابن عمر ؓ⁩ wept in the market of Medina at the news** — the editor: ⁨صحيح⁩. `[SOURCED]` ⁨سیر ج۳ ص۴۶۶⁩
- **⁨عائشہ ؓ⁩ to ⁨معاوية ؓ⁩**: `⁨أَقَتَلْتَ حُجْراً؟⁩` and his answer. `[SOURCED]` ⁨سیر ج۳ ص۴۶۶⁩–۴۶۷
- **⁨حُجْر الشر⁩, ⁨حجر بن يزيد⁩**, is his cousin and a different man — of ⁨علي ؓ⁩'s party, at the arbitration, later ⁨معاوية ؓ⁩'s governor of **⁨أرمينية⁩**. `[SOURCED]` ⁨سیر ج۳ ص۴۶۷⁩

**صعصعة بن صوحان**
- `⁨أَحَدُ خُطَبَاءِ العَرَبِ⁩ ... ⁨مِنْ كِبَارِ أَصْحَابِ عَلِيٍّ⁩`; **⁨ابن سعد⁩ declared him trustworthy**. `[SOURCED]` ⁨سیر ج۳ ص۵۲۸⁩–۵۲۹
- **His two brothers were killed at ⁨الجمل⁩ and he took the standard.** `[SOURCED]` ⁨سیر ج۳ ص۵۲۸⁩–۵۲۹
- **⁨زيد بن صوحان⁩'s burial instructions** at ⁨الجمل⁩: `⁨فَإِنِّي مُخَاصِمٌ، أُحَاجُّ يَوْمَ القِيَامَةِ⁩`. `[SOURCED]` ⁨سیر ج۳ ص۵۲۸⁩
- **He lived into ⁨معاوية ؓ⁩'s caliphate**; **no death date is given**. `[SOURCED]` ⁨سیر ج۳ ص۵۲۹⁩

**جرير بن عبد الله البجلي ؓ**
- `⁨الأَمِيْرُ، النَّبِيْلُ، الجَمِيْلُ⁩ ... ⁨مِنْ أَعْيَانِ الصَّحَابَةِ⁩`, of **⁨بجيلة⁩**, of **⁨قسر⁩**, of **⁨قحطان⁩**. `[SOURCED]` ⁨سیر ج۲ ص۵۳۰⁩
- The Prophet ﷺ: `⁨مِنْ خَيْرِ ذِي يَمَنٍ⁩ ... ⁨عَلَى وَجْهِهِ مِسْحَةَ مَلَكٍ⁩` — chain strong. `[SOURCED]` ⁨سیر ج۲ ص۵۳۱⁩
- He **destroyed ⁨ذو الخلصة⁩**; `⁨اللَّهُمَّ اجْعَلْهُ هَادِياً مَهْدِيّاً⁩` — ⁨البخاري⁩ and ⁨مسلم⁩. `[SOURCED]` ⁨سیر ج۲ ص۵۳۳⁩
- **⁨عمر ؓ⁩: `⁨جَرِيْرٌ يُوْسُفُ هَذِهِ الأُمَّةِ⁩`** — ⁨رجاله ثقات⁩. `[SOURCED]` ⁨سیر ج۲ ص۵۳۵⁩
- **Right wing of ⁨سعد ؓ⁩ at ⁨القادسية⁩**; **governor of ⁨همذان⁩ for ⁨عثمان ؓ⁩**, where he lost an eye. `[SOURCED]` ⁨سیر ج۲ ص۵۳۵⁩–۵۳۶
- He was **⁨علي ؓ⁩'s envoy to ⁨معاوية ؓ⁩**, and then **held aloof from both until he died**. `[SOURCED]` ⁨سیر ج۲ ص۵۳۵⁩–۵۳۶ (the withdrawal through ⁨الواقدي⁩ — attribute it)
- **Death 51 AH**, or **54** per ⁨ابن الكلبي⁩. `[SOURCED]` ⁨سیر ج۲ ص۵۳۶⁩
- His stated reason, `⁨فَلاَ أُقَاتِلُ مَنْ يَقُوْلُ⁩: ⁨لاَ إِلَهَ إِلاَّ اللهُ⁩` — **usable only with the chain-criticism said aloud.** `[SOURCED]` ⁨سیر ج۲ ص۵۳۶⁩

**الأشعث بن قيس ؓ**
- **His name was ⁨معدي كرب⁩**; ⁨الأشعث⁩ is a nickname from his hair. `[SOURCED]` ⁨سیر ج۲ ص۳۸⁩
- Of **⁨كندة⁩**; **brother-in-law of ⁨أبو بكر ؓ⁩** by ⁨فروة بنت أبي قحافة⁩; **his daughter was ⁨الحسن ؓ⁩'s wife**. `[SOURCED]` ⁨سیر ج۲ ص۳۹⁩ · ⁨ج۲ ص۴۲⁩
- **⁨آل عمران⁩ 77 was revealed over a case of his**, by his own statement — ⁨البخاري⁩ and ⁨مسلم⁩. `[SOURCED]` ⁨سیر ج۲ ص۳۸⁩
- **He apostatised in the ⁨ردة⁩, took safe conduct for seventy and not for himself**, and was pardoned. `[SOURCED]` ⁨سیر ج۲ ص۳۹⁩
- **The camel-market wedding feast** — chain sound. `[SOURCED]` ⁨سیر ج۲ ص۳۹⁩
- `⁨إِنَّ هَذَا لَمْ يَرْتَدَّ، وَإِنِّي ارْتَدَدْتُ⁩`. `[SOURCED]` ⁨سیر ج۲ ص۴۰⁩
- **He lost an eye at ⁨اليرموك⁩**; he was **the most senior of ⁨علي ؓ⁩'s commanders at ⁨صفين⁩**, on **the right wing**. `[SOURCED]` ⁨سیر ج۲ ص۳۸⁩ · ⁨ج۲ ص۴۰⁩
- **The water at ⁨صفين⁩**: two thousand against five thousand, and he took it. `[SOURCED]` ⁨سیر ج۲ ص۴۰⁩
- **His appeal to ⁨معاوية ؓ⁩ over the water, citing ⁨الحجرات⁩ 9**, and ⁨معاوية ؓ⁩'s order to release it. `[SOURCED]` ⁨سیر ج۲ ص۴۱⁩
- `⁨وَمَنْ لَكَ إِمَامٌ مِثْلُ عَلِيٍّ⁩` — ⁨البخاري⁩, ⁨التاريخ الكبير⁩. `[SOURCED]` ⁨سیر ج۲ ص۴۰⁩
- **Governor of ⁨أذربيجان⁩ for ⁨عثمان ؓ⁩.** `[SOURCED]` ⁨سیر ج۲ ص۴۱⁩
- **Died 40 AH, forty nights after ⁨علي ؓ⁩; ⁨الحسن ؓ⁩ prayed over him**; buried in his own house. `[SOURCED]` ⁨سیر ج۲ ص۴۲⁩
- His grandson **⁨عبد الرحمن بن محمد بن الأشعث⁩** fought ⁨الحجاج⁩ in, it is said, **eighty-one pitched battles**. `[SOURCED]` ⁨سیر ج۲ ص۴۲⁩–۴۳

**حذيفة بن اليمان ؓ**
- `⁨مِنْ نُجَبَاءِ أَصْحَابِ مُحَمَّدٍ⁩ -⁨صَلَّى اللَّهُ عَلَيْهِ وَسَلَّمَ⁩- ⁨وَهُوَ صَاحِبُ السِّرِّ⁩`. `[SOURCED]` ⁨سیر ج۲ ص۳۶۱⁩
- Of **⁨عبس⁩**, **allied to the Anṣār**, and counted `⁨مِنْ أَعْيَانِ المُهَاجِرِيْنَ⁩`. `[SOURCED]` ⁨سیر ج۲ ص۳۶۱⁩–۳۶۲
- **His father was killed by Muslims by mistake at ⁨أحد⁩**, and he gave the blood-money away as ṣadaqa. `[SOURCED]` ⁨سیر ج۲ ص۳۶۲⁩
- **He was not at ⁨بدر⁩** because of the oath Quraysh took from him; the Prophet ﷺ: `⁨نَفِيْ بِعَهْدِهِمْ⁩`. `[SOURCED]` ⁨سیر ج۲ ص۳۶۴⁩
- **He was the scout on the night of ⁨الأحزاب⁩.** `[SOURCED]` ⁨سیر ج۲ ص۳۶۴⁩
- **⁨عمر ؓ⁩ asked him whether he was among the hypocrites**, and the answer. `[SOURCED]` ⁨سیر ج۲ ص۳۶۴⁩
- **Governor of ⁨المدائن⁩ for ⁨عمر ؓ⁩**, and **still governing when ⁨عثمان ؓ⁩ was killed**; **⁨الدينور⁩ was opened at his hands**. `[SOURCED]` ⁨سیر ج۲ ص۳۶۳⁩–۳۶۴
- **⁨عمر ؓ⁩ lay in wait on the road to see him come back unchanged**: `⁨أَنْتَ أَخِي، وَأَنَا أَخُوْكَ⁩`. `[SOURCED]` ⁨سیر ج۲ ص۳۶۶⁩
- `⁨كُنْتُ أَسْأَلُهُ عَنِ الشَّرِّ، مَخَافَةَ أَنْ يُدْرِكَنِي⁩` — ⁨البخاري⁩. `[SOURCED]` ⁨سیر ج۲ ص۳۶۵⁩
- **⁨علي ؓ⁩ on him**: `⁨عَلِمَ المُنَافِقِيْنَ، وَسَأَلَ عَنِ المُعْضِلاَتِ⁩`. `[SOURCED]` ⁨سیر ج۲ ص۳۶۳⁩
- **Died at ⁨المدائن⁩ in 36 AH, forty nights after ⁨عثمان ؓ⁩**; his dying words, and the two white garments. `[SOURCED]` ⁨سیر ج۲ ص۳۶۴⁩ · ⁨ج۲ ص۳۶۶⁩ · ⁨ج۲ ص۳۶۸⁩–۳۶۹

**قيس بن سعد بن عبادة ؓ**
- `⁨سَيِّدُ الخَزْرَجِ وَابْنُ سَيِّدِهِم⁩` — **son of ⁨سعد بن عبادة ؓ⁩ of ⁨سقيفة بني ساعدة⁩**. `[SOURCED]` ⁨سیر ج۳ ص۱۰۲⁩
- **He stood to the Prophet ﷺ as the chief of police to the commander** — ⁨البخاري⁩. `[SOURCED]` ⁨سیر ج۳ ص۱۰۳⁩
- **Governor of Egypt for ⁨علي ؓ⁩, 36 AH to 37 AH**; he held it by manoeuvre and **sent no grain to Syria**. `[SOURCED]` ⁨سیر ج۳ ص۱۰۳⁩ · ⁨ج۳ ص۱۰۹⁩
- **⁨معاوية ؓ⁩ had a letter written in his name and read out in Damascus**, and ⁨علي ؓ⁩ removed him. `[SOURCED]` ⁨سیر ج۳ ص۱۰۹⁩
- **Egypt then passed to ⁨الأشتر⁩, who died on the road; to ⁨محمد بن أبي بكر ؓ⁩, who was killed; and then to ⁨عمرو ؓ⁩.** `[SOURCED]` ⁨سیر ج۳ ص۱۰۹⁩
- **Dismissed, he refused to leave ⁨علي ؓ⁩** and was made **commander of the vanguard**. `[SOURCED]` ⁨سیر ج۳ ص۱۱۰⁩
- **⁨معاوية ؓ⁩'s letter of reproach to ⁨مروان⁩ and ⁨الأسود⁩.** `[SOURCED]` ⁨سیر ج۳ ص۱۱۰⁩
- `⁨المَكْرُ وَالخَدِيْعَةُ فِي النَّارِ⁩` and `⁨لَكُنْتُ مِنْ أَمْكَرِ هَذِهِ الأُمَّةِ⁩`. `[SOURCED]` ⁨سیر ج۳ ص۱۰۷⁩–۱۰۸
- **After ⁨علي ؓ⁩'s death he took safe conduct for his five thousand and none for himself**, and slaughtered a camel a day for them as far as **⁨صِرار⁩**. `[SOURCED]` ⁨سیر ج۳ ص۱۱۰⁩–۱۱۱
- **Died at the end of ⁨معاوية ؓ⁩'s caliphate; no year is given.** `[SOURCED]` ⁨سیر ج۳ ص۱۱۲⁩

**Two cross-cutting sentences the whole session can lean on**
- **⁨الزهري⁩'s five men and their five choices** — the cleanest proof available that the division was not tribal, regional or hereditary. `[SOURCED]` ⁨سیر ج۳ ص۲۲⁩–۲۳
- **⁨الذهبي⁩'s own refusals** — `⁨مَا أَدْرِي مَا وَجْهُ هَذَا القَوْلِ⁩` (⁨ج۲ ص۳۹۴⁩), `⁨هَذَا مُنْقَطِعٌ⁩` (⁨ج۳ ص۱۱۱⁩), `⁨مُطَوَّلَةً بِلاَ سَنَدٍ⁩` (⁨ج۳ ص۲۷⁩), `⁨وَهَذَا خَطَأٌ⁩` (⁨ج۳ ص۷۷⁩). **These are the platform's licence to decline.** `[SOURCED]`

## 1. عمرو بن العاص ؓ

**The ⁨تراجم⁩ notice, in one breath**

- `name + ⁨لقب⁩` — **⁨عمرو بن العاص بن وائل السهمي ؓ⁩**, ⁨أبو عبد الله⁩. ⁨الذهبي رحمہ اللہ⁩ heads his entry
  **⁨الإمام⁩**, and calls him **⁨داهية قريش⁩** — the shrewdest head in Quraysh. `[SOURCED]` ⁨سیر ج۳ ص۵۴⁩–۵۵.
- `dates` — became Muslim **early in 8 AH**, months before the conquest of Mecca; died **on the night
  of ʿĪd al-Fiṭr**, in **43 AH** by most, **42** by others. `[SOURCED]` ⁨سیر ج۳ ص۵۵⁩ · ⁨ج۳ ص۷۷⁩.
  On the Line: from the eighth year of Hijra to the third year of Muʿāwiya's caliphate.
- `what he did` — he conquered Egypt for ⁨عمر ؓ⁩, governed it, lost it under ⁨عثمان ؓ⁩, sat out the
  first half of the fitna in Mecca, then joined ⁨معاوية ؓ⁩, was one of the two arbitrators at
  ⁨التحكيم⁩, and died governing Egypt again. `[SOURCED]` ⁨سیر ج۳ ص۵۸⁩ · ⁨ج۳ ص۷۱⁩–۷۳.

**Clan and kin.** ⁨بنو سهم⁩, one of the ⁨بطون⁩ of Quraysh — not one of the great houses, which is part of
why the room should notice how far he rose `[STANDARD]` *(to verify)*. ⁨الزبير بن بكار⁩ records that he
was the maternal brother of **⁨عروة بن أثاثة⁩**, one of the emigrants to Abyssinia `[SOURCED]` ⁨سیر⁩
⁨ج۳ ص۵۶⁩. His brother **⁨هشام بن العاص ؓ⁩** has his own ⁨ترجمه⁩ immediately after his; his son is
**⁨عبد الله بن عمرو ؓ⁩**, the ascetic and traditionist `[SOURCED]` ⁨سیر ج۳ ص۷۷⁩ ff. He was **older than
⁨عمر ؓ⁩** and used to say he remembered the night ⁨عمر⁩ was born `[SOURCED]` ⁨سیر ج۳ ص۵۸⁩.

**Before 23 AH, in three or four sentences.** He was one of the horsemen and champions of Quraysh in
the Jāhiliyya, and a good poet `[SOURCED]` ⁨سیر ج۳ ص۵۸⁩. He went twice to ⁨النجاشي⁩, the second time to
ask for Jaʿfar ؓ to be handed over, and came away a Muslim `[SOURCED]` ⁨سیر ج۳ ص۵۹⁩–۶۲. He reached
Medina in the company of ⁨خالد بن الوليد ؓ⁩ and ⁨عثمان بن طلحة ؓ⁩, and the Prophet ﷺ at once gave him
command `[SOURCED]` ⁨سیر ج۳ ص۵۵⁩. He commanded the expedition of **⁨ذات السلاسل⁩** with ⁨أبو بكر ؓ⁩ and
⁨عمر ؓ⁩ serving under him — a fact ⁨البخاري⁩ and ⁨مسلم⁩ both carry `[SOURCED]` ⁨سیر ج۳ ص۶۷⁩.

**In this window.** He fought at **⁨اليرموك⁩** and distinguished himself `[SOURCED]` ⁨سیر ج۳ ص۷۰⁩. ⁨خليفة⁩
records that ⁨عمر ؓ⁩ gave him ⁨فلسطين⁩ and ⁨الأردن⁩, then wrote to him to move on Egypt, sending ⁨الزبير ؓ⁩
after him as reinforcement `[SOURCED]` ⁨سیر ج۳ ص۷۰⁩. ⁨ابن لهيعة⁩: **Alexandria fell in 21 AH**, and the
city broke its treaty again in **25** `[SOURCED]` ⁨سیر ج۳ ص۷۰⁩. ⁨خليفة⁩: he took **⁨طرابلس الغرب⁩ in 24**,
and it is also said **23** `[SOURCED]` ⁨سیر ج۳ ص۷۰⁩. He governed Egypt for ⁨عمر ؓ⁩ and for the first part
of ⁨عثمان ؓ⁩'s caliphate; ⁨الزهري⁩ records that ⁨عثمان ؓ⁩ removed him and appointed
**⁨عبد الله بن أبي سرح⁩** `[SOURCED]` ⁨سیر ج۳ ص۵۸⁩ · ⁨ج۳ ص۷۱⁩. When the fitna came he held to Mecca until
the day of ⁨الجمل⁩, then consulted his two sons and went to ⁨معاوية ؓ⁩, who gave him Egypt `[SOURCED]`
⁨سیر ج۳ ص۷۱⁩–۷۲. He entered Egypt as its governor **in 39 AH**, stayed about three years, and died
there `[SOURCED]` ⁨سیر ج۳ ص۷۳⁩.

**One ⁨واقعہ⁩ worth telling — the two pulpits at Alexandria.** A Byzantine notable called across the
lines for a man to talk with. ⁨عمرو ؓ⁩ went out himself, each with an interpreter, and two pulpits were
set up. Asked who the Arabs were, he answered with the plainest self-description in the whole of this
window: we were the narrowest of people in land and the worst in living, eating carrion, raiding one
another — until a man rose among us who was neither our noblest nor our richest, and said, *I am the
Messenger of God to you*. The Byzantine laughed and said: your Messenger has told the truth, and
messengers came to us with the like; we kept to it until kings arose among us who did as they pleased
and left the affair of the prophets.

> فَإِنْ أَنْتُم أَخَذْتُمْ بِأَمْرِ نَبِيِّكُمْ، لَمْ يُقَاتِلْكُمْ أَحَدٌ إِلاَّ غَلَبْتُمُوْهُ، وَإِذَا فَعَلْتُم مِثْلَ الَّذِي فَعَلْنَا، فَتَرَكْتُمْ أَمَرَ نَبِيِّكُم، لَمْ تَكُوْنُوا أَكْثَرَ عَدَداً مِنَّا، وَلاَ أَشَدَّ مِنَّا قُوَّةً
> — ⁨سیر أعلام النبلاء ج۳ ص۷۱⁩ · https://shamela.ws/book/10906/2680
> *English:* "If you hold to the command of your Prophet, no one will fight you but you will beat
> him; and if you do as we did and abandon the command of your Prophet, you will be no more numerous
> than we were, nor any stronger."

`[SOURCED]` ⁨سیر ج۳ ص۷۰⁩–۷۱. ⁨الذهبي⁩ gives it from ⁨ابن عساكر⁩ with no grading of his own.

**His own words.**

> لاَ أَمَلُّ ثَوْبِي مَا وَسِعَنِي، وَلاَ أَمَلُّ زَوْجَتِي مَا أَحْسَنَتْ عِشْرَتِي، وَلاَ أَمَلُّ دَابَّتِي مَا حَمَلَتْنِي، إِنَّ المَلاَلَ مِنْ سَيِّئِ الأَخْلاَقِ.
> — ⁨سیر أعلام النبلاء ج۳ ص۵۷⁩ · https://shamela.ws/book/10906/2666
> *English:* "I do not tire of my garment while it still fits me, nor of my wife while she keeps
> good company with me, nor of my mount while it still carries me. Weariness is one of the evil traits."

> لَيْسَ العَاقِلُ مَنْ يَعْرِفُ الخَيْرَ مِنَ الشَّرِّ، وَلَكِنْ هُوَ الَّذِي يَعْرِفُ خَيْرَ الشَّرَّيْنِ.
> — ⁨سیر أعلام النبلاء ج۳ ص۷۴⁩ · https://shamela.ws/book/10906/2683
> *English:* "The intelligent man is not the one who tells good from evil, but the one who can tell
> the better of two evils."

> اللَّهُمَّ أَمَرْتَنَا فَتَرَكْنَا، وَنَهَيْتَنَا فَرَكِبْنَا، وَلاَ يَسَعُنَا إِلاَّ مَغْفِرَتُكَ.
> — ⁨سیر أعلام النبلاء ج۳ ص۷۵⁩ · https://shamela.ws/book/10906/2684
> *English:* "O God, You commanded us and we left it; You forbade us and we did it; nothing can hold
> us but Your forgiveness."

The printed edition's editor marks the last of these **⁨إسناده صحيح⁩** and notes it is in ⁨المسند⁩
۴/۱۹۹–۲۰۰ `[SOURCED]` ⁨سیر ج۳ ص۷۵⁩, footnote (٥).

**The date and manner of death.** ⁨الذهبي⁩ records the disagreement openly and settles one part of it
himself: they say he died **on the night of ʿĪd al-Fiṭr**; ⁨الليث⁩, ⁨الهيثم بن عدي⁩, ⁨الواقدي⁩ and others
say **43 AH**; ⁨محمد بن عبد الله بن نمير⁩ and others say **42**; ⁨يحيى بن بكير⁩ says **43, aged about a
hundred**; ⁨العجلي⁩ says **ninety-nine**; ⁨الواقدي⁩, from ⁨عمرو بن شعيب⁩, says **seventy, in 43** — and of
the report that he died in **51**, ⁨الذهبي⁩ says in his own voice: `⁨وَهَذَا خَطَأٌ⁩` `[SOURCED]` ⁨سیر⁩
⁨ج۳ ص۷۷⁩. He died in open fear, repeating ⁨لا إله إلا الله⁩ until he went `[SOURCED]` ⁨سیر ج۳ ص۷۵⁩–۷۶.

**⁨الذهبي⁩'s own summing-up — the single most useful sentence about him.**

> وَكَانَ مِنْ رِجَالِ قُرَيْشٍ رَأْياً، وَدَهَاءً، وَحَزْماً، وَكَفَاءةً، وَبَصَراً بِالحُرُوْبِ، وَمِنْ أَشْرَافِ مُلُوْكِ العَرَبِ، وَمِنْ أَعْيَانِ المُهَاجِرِيْنَ، وَاللهُ يَغْفِرُ لَهُ وَيَعفُو عَنْهُ، وَلَوْلاَ حُبُّهُ لِلدُّنْيَا، وَدُخُوْلُهُ فِي أُمُوْرٍ، لَصَلُحَ لِلْخِلاَفَةِ، فَإِنَّ لَهُ سَابِقَةً لَيْسَتْ لِمُعَاوِيَةَ.
> — ⁨سیر أعلام النبلاء ج۳ ص۵۹⁩ · https://shamela.ws/book/10906/2668
> *English:* "He was among the men of Quraysh in judgement, shrewdness, resolve, competence and
> insight into war; among the noblest of the kings of the Arabs and among the chief of the
> Muhājirūn — may God forgive him and pardon him. Had it not been for his love of this world and his
> entering into certain affairs, he would have been fit for the caliphate, for he has a precedence
> that ⁨معاوية⁩ does not have."

`[SOURCED]` ⁨سیر ج۳ ص۵۹⁩. This is the model the speaker follows all evening: praise and reservation in
the same breath, in the words of the authority, with no verdict of our own added.

**⚠ What not to say about him from the platform.** Do not use the exchange on ⁨صفين⁩ and ⁨عمار ؓ⁩
(⁨سیر ج۳ ص۶۸⁩), the black-standard report (same page), the ⁨شداد بن أوس⁩ report which ⁨ابن عساكر⁩ himself
declares to rest on two unknown narrators (⁨سیر ج۳ ص۷۲⁩), or the ⁨ابن عباس ؓ⁩ exchange in which each man
accuses the other over ⁨عثمان ؓ⁩ (⁨سیر ج۳ ص۷۳⁩). See **Not for the hall**. Never explain why he chose
⁨معاوية ؓ⁩ over ⁨علي ؓ⁩ beyond the words of the report itself; the report has him giving his own reason,
and that is where the speaker stops.

## 2. أبو موسى الأشعري ؓ

**The ⁨تراجم⁩ notice, in one breath**

- `name + ⁨لقب⁩` — **⁨عبد الله بن قيس بن سليم⁩, ⁨أبو موسى الأشعري ؓ⁩**. ⁨الذهبي⁩ heads him
  **⁨الإمام الكبير، صاحب رسول الله ﷺ، الفقيه، المقرئ⁩** `[SOURCED]` ⁨سیر ج۲ ص۳۸۰⁩–۳۸۱.
- `dates` — became Muslim at Mecca before the Hijra, reached Medina in the days of ⁨خيبر⁩ (7 AH);
  died, on ⁨الذهبي⁩'s own preferred reckoning, in **⁨ذو الحجة⁩ 44 AH** `[SOURCED]` ⁨سیر ج۲ ص۳۸۲⁩ ·
  ⁨ج۲ ص۳۹۸⁩. On the Line: from ⁨خيبر⁩ to the fourth year of Muʿāwiya's caliphate.
- `what he did` — he was the Prophet's ﷺ governor over ⁨زبيد⁩ and ⁨عدن⁩, ⁨عمر ؓ⁩'s governor of ⁨البصرة⁩ and
  the conqueror of ⁨الأهواز⁩, ⁨أصبهان⁩ and ⁨تستر⁩, the man who taught Basra its Qurʾān, and the arbitrator
  ⁨علي ؓ⁩ was pressed into appointing at ⁨التحكيم⁩ `[SOURCED]` ⁨سیر ج۲ ص۳۸۱⁩–۳۸۲ · ⁨ج۲ ص۳۹۰⁩–۳۹۱ · ⁨ج۲ ص۳۹۴⁩.

**Clan and kin.** Not Quraysh and not Anṣār: **⁨الأشعريون⁩**, a Yemeni tribe. ⁨الواقدي⁩ records that he
came to Mecca and made a ⁨حلف⁩ with **⁨أبو أُحيحة الأموي⁩** — his only Qurashī tie `[SOURCED]` ⁨سیر⁩
⁨ج۲ ص۴۰۰⁩. He came with a party of his own people and two brothers, **⁨أبو رهم⁩** and **⁨أبو عامر ؓ⁩**
(one narration says ⁨أبو بردة⁩); ⁨أبو عامر ؓ⁩ commanded at ⁨أوطاس⁩ and was killed there `[SOURCED]` ⁨سیر⁩
⁨ج۲ ص۳۸۳⁩ · ⁨ج۲ ص۳۸۵⁩. His son **⁨أبو بردة⁩** is the chief narrator of his life, and the man ⁨معاوية ؓ⁩ later
commended to ⁨يزيد⁩ `[SOURCED]` ⁨سیر ج۲ ص۴۰۱⁩.

**Before 23 AH.** ⁨أبو أحمد الحاكم⁩: he became Muslim at Mecca, then came with the people of the two
ships three days after ⁨خيبر⁩, and the Prophet ﷺ gave them a share `[SOURCED]` ⁨سیر ج۲ ص۳۸۲⁩. The
Prophet ﷺ said of the Ashʿarīs, `⁨لَكُمُ الهِجْرَةُ مَرَّتَيْنِ⁩` — you have emigrated twice
`[SOURCED]` ⁨سیر ج۲ ص۳۸۴⁩. He and ⁨معاذ ؓ⁩ were sent to Yemen, and ⁨مسروق⁩ counts him among the six to
whom judgement belonged among the Companions `[SOURCED]` ⁨سیر ج۲ ص۳۸۱⁩ · ⁨ج۲ ص۳۸۸⁩. He was short, thin
and thin-bearded, and no Companion had a finer voice `[SOURCED]` ⁨سیر ج۲ ص۳۸۳⁩.

**In this window.** ⁨خليفة⁩: he took ⁨البصرة⁩ **in 17 AH**, after ⁨المغيرة⁩, and when he took ⁨الأهواز⁩ he
left ⁨عمران بن حصين ؓ⁩ over Basra `[SOURCED]` ⁨سیر ج۲ ص۳۹۰⁩. He sent **⁨الهرمزان⁩** up to ⁨عمر ؓ⁩ from ⁨تستر⁩
with ⁨أنس ؓ⁩ `[SOURCED]` ⁨سیر ج۲ ص۳۹۱⁩. ⁨ابن إسحاق⁩: he marched from ⁨نهاوند⁩ and **took ⁨أصبهان⁩ in 23 AH**
`[SOURCED]` ⁨سیر ج۲ ص۳۹۱⁩. ⁨عمر ؓ⁩ wrote into his will that no governor of his was to be kept more than
a year — *and keep the Ashʿarī four* `[SOURCED]` ⁨سیر ج۲ ص۳۹۱⁩. ⁨الزهري⁩: ⁨عثمان ؓ⁩ removed him from
⁨البصرة⁩ and appointed **⁨عبد الله بن عامر بن كريز⁩**; he afterwards held **⁨الكوفة⁩**, and died there
`[SOURCED]` ⁨سیر ج۲ ص۳۸۲⁩ · ⁨ج۲ ص۳۹۰⁩. He came out of Basra with **six hundred dirhams** and nothing
else — his family's stipend `[SOURCED]` ⁨سیر ج۲ ص۳۹۱⁩ · ⁨ج۲ ص۳۹۸⁩.

**⁨التحكيم⁩, in ⁨الذهبي⁩'s own words and no one else's.** This is the passage the speaker should carry
verbatim, because it states the sequence and nothing beyond it:

> وَلاَ رَيْبَ أَنَّ غُلاَةَ الشِّيْعَةِ يُبْغِضُوْنَ أَبَا مُوْسَى -رَضِيَ اللهُ عَنْهُ- لِكَوْنِهِ مَا قَاتَلَ مَعَ عَلِيٍّ، ثُمَّ لَمَّا حَكَّمَهُ عَلِيٌّ عَلَى نَفْسِهِ عَزَلَهُ، وَعَزَلَ مُعَاوِيَةَ، وَأَشَارَ بِابْنِ عُمَرَ؛ فَمَا انْتَظَمَ مِنْ ذَلِكَ حَالٌ.
> — ⁨سیر أعلام النبلاء ج۲ ص۳۹۴⁩ · https://shamela.ws/book/10906/2374
> *English:* "There is no doubt that the extreme Shīʿa hold ⁨أبو موسى ؓ⁩ in hatred, because he did not
> fight alongside ⁨علي⁩, and then, when ⁨علي⁩ made him arbitrator over his own case, he deposed him and
> deposed ⁨معاوية⁩ and proposed ⁨ابن عمر⁩ — and no settled state of affairs came out of that."

`[SOURCED]` ⁨سیر ج۲ ص۳۹۴⁩. And after the whole account, ⁨الذهبي⁩ gives his own verdict on the man:

> قُلْتُ: قَدْ كَانَ أَبُو مُوْسَى صَوَّاماً، قَوَّاماً، رَبَّانِيّاً، زَاهِداً، عَابِداً، مِمَّنْ جَمَعَ العِلْمَ وَالعَمَلَ وَالجِهَادَ وَسَلاَمَةَ الصَّدْرِ، لَمْ تُغَيِّرْهُ الإِمَارَةُ، وَلاَ اغْتَرَّ بِالدُّنْيَا.
> — ⁨سیر أعلام النبلاء ج۲ ص۳۹۶⁩ · https://shamela.ws/book/10906/2376
> *English:* "I say: ⁨أبو موسى⁩ was much-fasting, much-praying, a man of God, ascetic, devoted — one
> who combined knowledge, practice, jihād and a sound heart. Authority did not change him, and this
> world did not deceive him."

`[SOURCED]` ⁨سیر ج۲ ص۳۹۶⁩.

**One ⁨واقعہ⁩ worth telling — the letter he did not answer.** ⁨معاوية ؓ⁩ wrote to him, in his own hand,
saying that ⁨عمرو بن العاص⁩ had already given him his pledge, and swearing that if ⁨أبو موسى⁩ did the
same, one of his two sons would have ⁨الكوفة⁩ and the other ⁨البصرة⁩, no door would ever be shut against
him and no need of his go unmet — and asked for a reply in his own hand. ⁨أبو موسى ؓ⁩ wrote back four
lines.

> أَمَّا بَعْدُ، فَإِنَّكَ كَتَبْتَ إِلَيَّ فِي جَسِيْمِ أَمْرِ الأُمَّةِ، فَمَاذَا أَقُوْلُ لِرَبِّي إِذَا قَدِمْتُ عَلَيْهِ، لَيْسَ لِي فِيْمَا عَرَضْتَ مِنْ حَاجَةٍ، وَالسَّلاَمُ عَلَيْكَ.
> — ⁨سیر أعلام النبلاء ج۲ ص۳۹۶⁩ · https://shamela.ws/book/10906/2376
> *English:* "To proceed: you have written to me about a grave matter of this Umma. What shall I say
> to my Lord when I come before Him? I have no need of what you have offered. And peace be upon you."

The editor of the printed edition notes the same report in ⁨ابن سعد ۴⁩/۱۱۱–۱۱۲ through three separate
routes and calls it `⁨سند صحيح⁩` `[SOURCED]` ⁨سیر ج۲ ص۳۹۶⁩, footnote (٢). ⁨أبو بردة⁩ adds the ending: when
⁨معاوية ؓ⁩ did come to power, no door was shut against *him*, and no need of his went unmet
`[SOURCED]` ⁨سیر ج۲ ص۳۹۶⁩.

**His own words.**

> اللَّهُمَّ أَنْتَ المُؤْمِنُ تُحِبُّ المُؤْمِنَ، وَأَنْتَ المُهَيْمِنُ تُحِبُّ المُهَيْمِنَ، وَأَنْتَ السَّلاَمُ تُحِبُّ السَّلاَمَ.
> — ⁨سیر أعلام النبلاء ج۲ ص۳۹۳⁩ · https://shamela.ws/book/10906/2373
> *English:* "O God, You are the Believing One and You love the believer; You are the Guardian and
> You love the guardian; You are Peace and You love peace."

> إِنَّ الخَيْلَ إِذَا أُرْسِلَتْ فَقَارَبَتْ رَأْسَ مَجْرَاهَا، أَخْرَجَتْ جَمِيْعَ مَا عِنْدَهَا؛ وَالَّذِي بَقِيَ مِنْ أَجَلِي أَقَلُّ مِنْ ذَلِكَ.
> — ⁨سیر أعلام النبلاء ج۲ ص۳۹۳⁩ · https://shamela.ws/book/10906/2373
> *English:* "When horses are let loose and come near the end of the course, they bring out
> everything they have. What is left of my appointed term is less than that."

Said near the end, when he was told to spare himself. `[SOURCED]` ⁨سیر ج۲ ص۳۹۳⁩.

**The date and manner of death.** ⁨الذهبي⁩ lays the disagreement out and then states his own
preference: ⁨ابن سعد⁩ and ⁨أبو أحمد الحاكم⁩, **42 AH**; it is also said **43**; ⁨أبو نعيم⁩,
⁨ابن أبي شيبة⁩, ⁨ابن نمير⁩ and ⁨قعنب بن المحرر⁩, **44**; ⁨الواقدي⁩, **52**; ⁨المدائني⁩, **53, after ⁨المغيرة⁩** —
and ⁨الذهبي⁩: *"I said in ⁨طبقات القراء⁩: he died in ⁨ذو الحجة⁩, 44, and that is the sound view"*
`[SOURCED]` ⁨سیر ج۲ ص۳۹۷⁩–۳۹۸.

**⚠ What not to say about him from the platform.** Do not use the report in which ⁨حذيفة ؓ⁩ is made to
say of two men entering the mosque that one of them is a hypocrite — ⁨الذهبي⁩ himself does not accept
it (see **Not for the hall**). Do not attribute a motive to his arbitration. Do not say who "won"
⁨التحكيم⁩; ⁨الذهبي⁩'s own sentence is `⁨فَمَا انْتَظَمَ مِنْ ذَلِكَ حَالٌ⁩`, and the speaker says exactly
that much.

---

## 3. المغيرة بن شعبة ؓ

**The ⁨تراجم⁩ notice, in one breath**

- `name + ⁨لقب⁩` — **⁨المغيرة بن شعبة بن أبي عامر الثقفي ؓ⁩**, ⁨الأمير أبو عيسى⁩ — the kunya the Prophet ﷺ
  gave him and ⁨عمر ؓ⁩ later changed to ⁨أبو عبد الله⁩. In Kufa they called him **⁨مغيرة الرأي⁩**
  `[SOURCED]` ⁨سیر ج۳ ص۲۱⁩ · ⁨ج۳ ص۲۲⁩ · ⁨ج۳ ص۲۳⁩.
- `dates` — present at **⁨بيعة الرضوان⁩** (6 AH); died **⁨شعبان⁩ 50 AH, aged seventy**, in office as
  governor of ⁨الكوفة⁩ `[SOURCED]` ⁨سیر ج۳ ص۲۱⁩ · ⁨ج۳ ص۳۲⁩. On the Line: from ⁨الحديبية⁩ to the tenth year
  of Muʿāwiya's caliphate.
- `what he did` — he carried the Muslims' embassy to the Persian commander at ⁨القادسية⁩, governed
  ⁨البحرين⁩, ⁨البصرة⁩ and ⁨الكوفة⁩, fought at ⁨اليرموك⁩ where he lost an eye, withdrew from both sides in the
  fitna, led the Ḥajj in the year ⁨علي ؓ⁩ was killed, and then governed ⁨الكوفة⁩ for ⁨معاوية ؓ⁩ until he
  died `[SOURCED]` ⁨سیر ج۳ ص۲۱⁩ · ⁨ج۳ ص۲۷⁩ · ⁨ج۳ ص۲۹⁩–۳۰ · ⁨ج۳ ص۳۲⁩.

**Clan and kin.** **⁨ثقيف⁩** of ⁨الطائف⁩ — the **⁨الأحلاف⁩** half of ⁨ثقيف⁩, not **⁨بنو مالك⁩**; that split is
the whole of his early story `[SOURCED]` ⁨سیر ج۳ ص۲۴⁩–۲۵. His paternal uncle is
**⁨عروة بن مسعود الثقفي ؓ⁩**, the man Quraysh sent to negotiate at ⁨الحديبية⁩ `[SOURCED]` ⁨سیر ج۳ ص۲۵⁩. He
married **four daughters of ⁨أبو سفيان⁩** `[SOURCED]` ⁨سیر ج۳ ص۳۰⁩. And it is in his case that
**⁨زياد بن أبيه⁩** first appears in the record — the fourth witness who did not testify `[SOURCED]`
سیر ج۳ ص۲۷.

**Before 23 AH.** He was a keeper of the idol ⁨اللات⁩ before Islam, and says so of himself: *"we held
fast to our religion, and we were the servants of ⁨اللات⁩"* `[SOURCED]` ⁨سیر ج۳ ص۲۴⁩. He went to Egypt
with a party of ⁨بنو مالك⁩, was slighted there, killed thirteen of them and came to Medina with their
spoils and his Islam — and was refused the spoils to his face `[SOURCED]` ⁨سیر ج۳ ص۲۴⁩–۲۵. He stood
over the Prophet ﷺ in armour at ⁨الحديبية⁩ while his own uncle negotiated, and told him to take his
hand off the Prophet's ﷺ beard `[SOURCED]` ⁨سیر ج۳ ص۲۵⁩. He was the last man out of the Prophet's ﷺ
grave — he dropped his ring in so that he could be told to go back down for it `[SOURCED]` ⁨سیر⁩
⁨ج۳ ص۲۶⁩. He lost an eye — at **⁨اليرموك⁩**, or **⁨القادسية⁩**, or **at ⁨الطائف⁩**, or, ⁨عائشہ ؓ⁩ is made to
say, **looking at an eclipse**. ⁨الذهبي⁩ records all four without choosing `[SOURCED]` ⁨سیر ج۳ ص۲۱⁩ ·
ج۳ ص۲۳–۲۴ · ج۳ ص۳۲.

**In this window.** ⁨عمر ؓ⁩ set him over **⁨البحرين⁩**; the people disliked him and ⁨عمر ؓ⁩ removed him;
their chief then tried to keep him removed by carrying a bribe to ⁨عمر ؓ⁩ against him, and ⁨المغيرة⁩
turned the trick inside out in a single sentence `[SOURCED]` ⁨سیر ج۳ ص۲۶⁩–۲۷. He prayed for the people
at ⁨البصرة⁩ after ⁨عتبة بن غزوان⁩ left, and on ⁨عتبة⁩'s death ⁨عمر ؓ⁩ wrote him the governorship of
**⁨البصرة⁩**, which he held **three years** `[SOURCED]` ⁨سیر ج۳ ص۲۷⁩. He was then removed from ⁨البصرة⁩ and
given **⁨الكوفة⁩** — the turn that produced the proverb ⁨ابن سيرين⁩ records `[SOURCED]` ⁨سیر ج۳ ص۲۸⁩.
⁨الليث⁩: **⁨أذربيجان⁩ fell in 22 AH and he was its commander**; and it is said he took **⁨همذان⁩** by force
`[SOURCED]` ⁨سیر ج۳ ص۲۸⁩. He was the first man to be greeted with the formula of command from the
pulpit `[SOURCED]` ⁨سیر ج۳ ص۲۸⁩.

⁨الزهري⁩'s list of the five shrewdest heads in the fitna places him precisely:

> كَانَ دُهَاةُ النَّاسِ فِي الفِتْنَةِ خَمْسَةً، فَمِنْ قُرَيْشٍ: عَمْرٌو، وَمُعَاوِيَةُ، وَمِنَ الأَنْصَارِ: قَيْسُ بنُ سَعْدٍ، وَمِنْ ثَقِيْفٍ:
> — ⁨سیر أعلام النبلاء ج۳ ص۲۲⁩ · https://shamela.ws/book/10906/2631
> المُغِيْرَةُ، وَمِنَ المُهَاجِرِيْنَ: عَبْدُ اللهِ بنُ بُدَيْلِ بنِ وَرْقَاءَ الخُزَاعِيُّ. فَكَانَ مَعَ عَلِيٍّ: قَيْسٌ، وَابْنُ بُدَيْلٍ، وَاعْتَزَلَ المُغِيْرَةُ بنُ شُعْبَةَ (١) .
> — ⁨سیر أعلام النبلاء ج۳ ص۲۳⁩ · https://shamela.ws/book/10906/2632
> *English:* "The shrewdest of people in the fitna were five: from Quraysh, ⁨عمرو⁩ and ⁨معاوية⁩; from
> the Anṣār, ⁨قيس بن سعد⁩; from ⁨ثقيف⁩, ⁨المغيرة⁩; and from the Muhājirūn,
> ⁨عبد الله بن بديل بن ورقاء الخزاعي⁩. ⁨قيس⁩ and ⁨ابن بديل⁩ were with ⁨علي⁩, and ⁨المغيرة بن شعبة⁩ withdrew."

`[SOURCED]` ⁨سیر ج۳ ص۲۲⁩–۲۳. (The same list stands, with ⁨معاوية⁩ at its head, in ⁨قيس بن سعد⁩'s ⁨ترجمه⁩:
⁨سیر ج۳ ص۱۰۸⁩.) He withdrew to ⁨اليمن⁩, then to ⁨الطائف⁩, and when neither side sent anyone to the Ḥajj
season, he came and prayed with the people **in the year 40** and invoked for ⁨معاوية ؓ⁩ `[SOURCED]`
سیر ج۳ ص۲۹ (الليث: `وَحَجَّ بِالنَّاسِ المُغِيْرَةُ سَنَةَ أَرْبَعِيْنَ`).

**One ⁨واقعہ⁩ worth telling — the embassy at ⁨القادسية⁩.** On the day of ⁨القادسية⁩ he went out with ten
men to the Persian commander, who told him the Persians did not wish to kill them because they would
defile the ground. ⁨المغيرة ؓ⁩ answered that they had worshipped stones until God sent them a
Messenger, that they had not come for food, that they had found in the Persians' land food and water
in plenty — and that they would not leave until it belonged to both. The Persian said: *he has told
the truth.* And ⁨المغيرة ؓ⁩ said: *and tomorrow your eye will be put out* — and his own eye was put out
the next day by an arrow `[SOURCED]` ⁨سیر ج۳ ص۳۲⁩.

**His own words.**

> إِنَّ المَعْرِفَةَ تَنْفَعُ عِنْدَ الجَمَلِ الصَّؤُوْلِ وَالكَلْبِ العَقُوْرِ، فَكَيْفَ بِالمُسْلِمِ.
> — ⁨سیر أعلام النبلاء ج۳ ص۳۱⁩ · https://shamela.ws/book/10906/2640
> *English:* "Acquaintance counts for something even with a savage camel and a biting dog — how much
> more with a Muslim." (Said when he was told he showed favour to people he knew.)

And two sentences said about him. ⁨عمرو بن العاص ؓ⁩, advising ⁨معاوية ؓ⁩ to make him governor of ⁨الكوفة⁩:

> المُغِيْرَةُ، وَاسْتَغْنِ بِرَأْيِهِ، وَقُوَّتِهِ عَنِ المَكِيْدَةِ، وَاعْزِلْهُ عَنِ المَالِ، قَدْ كَانَ قَبْلَكَ عُمَرُ، وَعُثْمَانُ، فَفَعَلاَ ذَلِكَ.
> — ⁨سیر أعلام النبلاء ج۳ ص۳۰⁩ · https://shamela.ws/book/10906/2639
> *English:* "⁨المغيرة⁩. Use his judgement and his strength instead of stratagem — and keep him away
> from the treasury. ⁨عمر⁩ and ⁨عثمان⁩ were before you, and they did exactly that."

And ⁨جرير بن عبد الله ؓ⁩, standing up on the day he died:

> أُوْصِيْكُم بِتَقْوَى اللهِ، وَأَنْ تَسْمَعُوا وَتُطِيْعُوا حَتَّى يَأْتِيَكُم أَمِيْرٌ، اسْتَغْفِرُوا لِلْمُغِيْرَةِ، غَفَرَ اللهُ لَهُ، فَإِنَّهُ كَانَ يُحِبُّ العَافِيَةَ.
> — ⁨سیر أعلام النبلاء ج۳ ص۳۱⁩ · https://shamela.ws/book/10906/2640
> *English:* "I charge you with the fear of God, and that you listen and obey until a governor comes
> to you. Ask forgiveness for ⁨المغيرة⁩ — may God forgive him — for he loved to be let alone."

`[SOURCED]` ⁨سیر ج۳ ص۳۱⁩. (⁨أبو عوانة⁩'s wording has `⁨كَانَ يُحِبُّ العَفْوَ⁩` — "he loved to pardon" —
and ⁨الذهبي⁩ records both.)

**The date and manner of death.** `⁨وَقَالَ الجَمَاعَةُ⁩: ⁨مَاتَ أَمِيْرُ الكُوْفَةِ؛ المُغِيْرَةُ فِي سَنَةِ خَمْسِيْنَ، فِي شَعْبَانَ، وَلَهُ سَبْعُوْنَ سَنَةً⁩` — ⁨شعبان⁩ 50 AH, aged seventy, in office
`[SOURCED]` ⁨سیر ج۳ ص۳۲⁩. ⁨زياد⁩ stood over his grave and recited two lines of ⁨مهلهل⁩'s lament for his
brother ⁨كليب⁩ `[SOURCED]` ⁨سیر ج۳ ص۳۲⁩.

**⚠ What not to say about him from the platform.** Three things, all real and all barred. **(a)** The
charge brought against him at ⁨البصرة⁩ and ⁨عمر ؓ⁩'s judgement on it — see **Not for the hall**; ⁨الذهبي⁩
himself notes that the long version rests on ⁨سيف بن عمر⁩ and ⁨أبو حذيفة النجاري⁩ **with no chain at
all**. **(b)** The line `⁨كَانَ المُغِيْرَةُ يَنَالُ فِي خُطْبَتِهِ مِنْ عَلِيٍّ⁩` (⁨سیر ج۳ ص۳۱⁩). **(c)**
His own sentence about ⁨عثمان ؓ⁩ in the exchange with ⁨عمار ؓ⁩ (⁨سیر ج۳ ص۲۹⁩). The *withdrawal* he urged on
⁨عمار ؓ⁩, and ⁨عمار ؓ⁩'s refusal, may be told; the sentence about ⁨عثمان ؓ⁩ may not. And when his counsel to
⁨علي ؓ⁩ is told, it is told as counsel that was not taken, with no suggestion that either man was in
the wrong.

---

## 4. الأشتر مالك بن الحارث النخعي

**The ⁨تراجم⁩ notice, in one breath**

- `name + ⁨لقب⁩` — **⁨مالك بن الحارث النخعي⁩**, known as **⁨الأشتر⁩**. ⁨الذهبي⁩ heads his entry
  **⁨مَلِكُ العَرَبِ⁩ ... ⁨أَحَدُ الأَشْرَافِ وَالأَبْطَالِ المَذْكُوْرِيْنَ⁩** — one of the noted
  chiefs and champions `[SOURCED]` ⁨سیر ج۴ ص۳۴⁩. **He is not a Companion**: ⁨الذهبي⁩ lists his teachers
  as ⁨عمر ؓ⁩ and ⁨خالد بن الوليد ؓ⁩, which places him one rank below. No ؓ.
- `dates` — his birth is not given on this page `[STANDARD]` *(to verify)*; he lost an eye at
  **⁨اليرموك⁩** (15 AH) and **died on the road to Egypt after ⁨صفين⁩**, poisoned — which the sources put
  in **38 AH** `[SOURCED]` ⁨سیر ج۴ ص۳۴⁩ for the manner; `[CONVENTIONAL-ESTIMATE]` for the year, which
  this page does not state. On the Line: between ⁨صفين⁩ and the killing of ⁨محمد بن أبي بكر⁩.
- `what he did` — he was the most formidable field commander ⁨علي ؓ⁩ had, he came within reach of
  breaking ⁨معاوية ؓ⁩'s line at ⁨صفين⁩, and he was stopped not by the enemy but by his own side
  `[SOURCED]` ⁨سیر ج۴ ص۳۴⁩.

**Clan and kin.** **⁨النخع⁩**, a clan of ⁨مذحج⁩ — a Yemeni tribe settled in ⁨الكوفة⁩, not Quraysh and not
Anṣār. That matters: the men who decided ⁨صفين⁩ on ⁨علي ؓ⁩'s side were largely the tribal notables of
Kufa `[SOURCED]` ⁨سیر ج۴ ص۳۴⁩ for the nisba. His son **⁨إبراهيم بن الأشتر⁩** is the man who killed
**⁨عبيد الله بن زياد⁩** at the battle of **⁨الخازر⁩**, later served ⁨مصعب بن الزبير⁩, and was killed with
him in **72 AH** — ⁨الذهبي⁩ calls him `⁨شِيْعِيّاً، فَاضِلاً⁩` `[SOURCED]` ⁨سیر ج۴ ص۳۵⁩.

**Before 23 AH.** Very little on this page, and the note does not invent any: he narrated from ⁨عمر ؓ⁩
and from ⁨خالد بن الوليد ؓ⁩, and his eye was put out at **⁨اليرموك⁩** `[SOURCED]` ⁨سیر ج۴ ص۳۴⁩. ⁨عمر ؓ⁩ is
recorded as having looked him up and down and said what the room should hear before anything else
about him.

**In this window.** ⁨الذهبي⁩'s own sentence is the whole of his role, and it is unflattering and
admiring at once:

> وَكَانَ شَهْماً، مُطَاعاً، زَعِراً (١) ، أَلَبَّ عَلَى عُثْمَانَ، وَقَاتَلَهُ، وَكَانَ ذَا فَصَاحَةٍ وَبَلاَغَةٍ.
> — ⁨سیر أعلام النبلاء ج۴ ص۳۴⁩ · https://shamela.ws/book/10906/3187
> *English:* "He was sharp, obeyed, ill-tempered; he stirred up against ⁨عثمان⁩ and fought him; and he
> had eloquence and rhetoric."

`[SOURCED]` ⁨سیر ج۴ ص۳۴⁩. This is ⁨الذهبي⁩'s own wording and it is quoted as his, never as ours. Then
صفين:

> شَهِدَ صِفِّيْنَ (٢) مَعَ عَلِيٍّ، وَتَمَّيْزَ يَوْمَئِذٍ، وَكَادَ أَنْ يَهْزِمَ مُعَاوِيَةَ، فَحَمَلَ عَلَيْهِ أَصْحَابُ عَلِيٍّ لَمَّا رَأَوْا مُصْحَفَ جُنْدِ الشَّامِ عَلَى الأَسِنَّةِ يَدْعُوْنَ إِلَى كِتَابِ اللهِ. وَمَا أَمْكَنَهُ مُخَالَفَةُ عَلِيٍّ، فَكَفَّ.
> — ⁨سیر أعلام النبلاء ج۴ ص۳۴⁩ · https://shamela.ws/book/10906/3187
> *English:* "He was present at ⁨صفين⁩ with ⁨علي⁩ and distinguished himself that day, and came near to
> routing ⁨معاوية⁩ — and then ⁨علي⁩'s own companions bore down on *him*, when they saw the Muṣḥaf of the
> Syrian army raised on the spear-points calling to the Book of God. It was not open to him to
> disobey ⁨علي⁩, so he held back."

`[SOURCED]` ⁨سیر ج۴ ص۳۴⁩. This single sentence carries the raising of the maṣāḥif, the split inside
⁨علي ؓ⁩'s own camp, and the reason ⁨التحكيم⁩ happened at all — and it does so in a Sunni authority's own
words, which is exactly what this evening needs.

**One ⁨واقعہ⁩ worth telling — ⁨عمر ؓ⁩ looks at him.**

> إِنَّ لِلْمُسْلِمِيْنَ مِنْ هَذَا يَوْماً عَصِيْباً.
> — ⁨سیر أعلام النبلاء ج۴ ص۳۴⁩ · https://shamela.ws/book/10906/3187
> *English:* "The Muslims will have a hard day from this man."

⁨عبد الله بن سلمة المرادي⁩ reports that ⁨عمر ؓ⁩ looked ⁨الأشتر⁩ up and down and said it `[SOURCED]` ⁨سیر⁩
⁨ج۴ ص۳۴⁩. Told plainly and without embroidery, it is one of the strongest openings in the session.

**The date and manner of death, with the disagreement.** When ⁨علي ؓ⁩ came back from ⁨صفين⁩ he fitted
⁨الأشتر⁩ out as governor of Egypt, and he **died on the road, poisoned**; it is said that a slave of
⁨عثمان ؓ⁩'s met him and poisoned honey for him `[SOURCED]` ⁨سیر ج۴ ص۳۴⁩. ⁨الذهبي⁩ adds that ⁨علي ؓ⁩ used to
find him hard going, because he was difficult to handle — and then, when the death notice reached
him, said:

> إِنَّا للهِ، مَالِكٌ، وَمَا مَالِكٌ! وَهَلْ مَوْجُوْدٌ مِثْلُ ذَلِكَ؟! لَوْ كَانَ حَدِيْداً لَكَانَ قَيْداً، وَلَوْ كَانَ حَجَراً لَكَانَ صَلْداً، عَلَى مِثْلِهِ فَلْتَبْكِ البَوَاكِي.
> — ⁨سیر أعلام النبلاء ج۴ ص۳۴⁩ · https://shamela.ws/book/10906/3187
> *English:* "To God we belong. Mālik — and what a Mālik! Is the like of that to be found? Had he
> been iron he would have been a fetter; had he been stone he would have been solid rock. For such a
> man let the mourning women weep."

`[SOURCED]` ⁨سیر ج۴ ص۳۴⁩. And on the other side, ⁨عمرو بن العاص ؓ⁩ was glad of his death and said
`⁨إِنَّ للهِ جُنُوْداً مِنْ عَسَلٍ⁩` — "God has armies made of honey" `[SOURCED]` ⁨سیر ج۴ ص۳۵⁩.

**A disagreement worth recording.** ⁨الذهبي⁩ also reports, with `⁨وَقِيْلَ⁩` — *it is said* — that
⁨ابن الزبير ؓ⁩ fought a long single combat with ⁨الأشتر⁩ and called out the line
`⁨اقْتُلُوْنِي وَمَالِكاً⁩ ... ⁨وَاقْتُلُوا مَالِكاً مَعِي⁩` `[SOURCED]` ⁨سیر ج۴ ص۳۵⁩. The printed
edition's editor notes that in **⁨الطبري ۴⁩/۵۲۰** the same line is put in the mouth of
**⁨عبد الرحمن بن عتاب بن أسيد⁩ at ⁨الجمل⁩**, not ⁨ابن الزبير ؓ⁩ — so the attribution is not fixed. Do not
build a scene on it.

**⚠ What not to say about him from the platform.** ⁨الذهبي⁩'s `⁨أَلَبَّ عَلَى عُثْمَانَ، وَقَاتَلَهُ⁩`
is quoted as ⁨الذهبي⁩'s judgement, in his name, and left there. No detail of the siege of ⁨عثمان ؓ⁩, no
list of who did what, no verdict.

---

## 5. محمد بن أبي بكر ؓ

**The ⁨تراجم⁩ notice, in one breath**

- `name + ⁨لقب⁩` — **⁨محمد بن أبي بكر الصديق ؓ⁩**. ⁨الذهبي⁩ gives him no ⁨لقب⁩, and none is invented here
  `[SOURCED]` ⁨سیر ج۳ ص۴۸۱⁩.
- `dates` — **born to ⁨أسماء بنت عميس ؓ⁩ during ⁨حجة الوداع⁩, at the moment of iḥrām** (10 AH); **killed
  in Egypt**, in or just after **⁨رمضان⁩ 37 AH**, when he had held Egypt only months `[SOURCED]` ⁨سیر⁩
  ⁨ج۳ ص۴۸۲⁩. On the Line: born at the Farewell Pilgrimage, killed between ⁨صفين⁩ and ⁨التحكيم⁩.
- `what he did` — he was ⁨عثمان ؓ⁩'s appointee to Egypt, then one of those who rose against him, then
  ⁨علي ؓ⁩'s governor of Egypt, and he was killed there when ⁨معاوية ؓ⁩'s forces took the province
  `[SOURCED]` ⁨سیر ج۳ ص۴۸۲⁩.

**Clan and kin.** **⁨بنو تيم⁩** of Quraysh, the house of ⁨الصديق ؓ⁩. Son of **⁨أبو بكر الصديق ؓ⁩**; his
mother **⁨أسماء بنت عميس ؓ⁩** had been the wife of ⁨جعفر بن أبي طالب ؓ⁩ and, after ⁨أبو بكر ؓ⁩, married
**⁨علي ؓ⁩** — so he grew up in ⁨علي ؓ⁩'s household `[SOURCED]` ⁨سیر ج۳ ص۴۸۲⁩ for the birth;
`[STANDARD]` *(to verify)* for the marriage sequence, which this page does not give. His half-sister
is ⁨أم المؤمنين⁩ **⁨عائشہ ؓ⁩**; his son is **⁨القاسم بن محمد⁩** the jurist, one of the seven fuqahāʾ of
Medina, whom ⁨الذهبي⁩ names on the same page `[SOURCED]` ⁨سیر ج۳ ص۴۸۲⁩. This is the kinship line the room
must see: **⁨الصديق ؓ⁩ → ⁨محمد⁩ → ⁨القاسم الفقيه⁩** — and it runs straight through the fitna.

**Before 23 AH.** He is a child in this window: born at the Farewell Pilgrimage, he was about
twenty-five when ⁨عثمان ؓ⁩ was killed `[SOURCED]` ⁨سیر ج۳ ص۴۸۲⁩ for the birth;
`[CONVENTIONAL-ESTIMATE]` for the arithmetic.

**In this window.** ⁨الذهبي⁩'s sentence is short, and it says three things:

> وَكَانَ قَدْ وَلاَّهُ عُثْمَانُ إِمْرَةَ مِصْرَ، كَمَا هُوَ مُبَيَّنٌ فِي سِيْرَةِ عُثْمَانَ، ثُمَّ سَارَ لِحِصَارِ عُثْمَانَ، وَفَعَلَ أَمْراً كَبِيْراً، فَكَانَ أَحَدَ مَنْ تَوَثَّبَ عَلَى عُثْمَانَ حَتَّى قُتِلَ، ثُمَّ انْضَمَّ إِلَى عَلِيٍّ، فَكَانَ مِنْ أُمَرَائِهِ، فَسَيَّرَهُ عَلَى إِمْرَةِ مِصْرَ، سَنَةَ سَبْعٍ وَثَلاَثِيْنَ، فِي رَمَضَانِهَا.
> — ⁨سیر أعلام النبلاء ج۳ ص۴۸۲⁩ · https://shamela.ws/book/10906/3090
> *English:* "⁨عثمان⁩ had appointed him to the governorship of Egypt, as is set out in the life of
> ⁨عثمان⁩; then he went out for the siege of ⁨عثمان⁩ and did a grave thing, and he was one of those who
> rose against ⁨عثمان⁩ until he was killed. Then he attached himself to ⁨علي⁩ and was one of his
> commanders, and ⁨علي⁩ despatched him to the governorship of Egypt in the year 37, in its Ramaḍān."

`[SOURCED]` ⁨سیر ج۳ ص۴۸۲⁩. ⁨الذهبي⁩'s own closing comment, made two pages earlier about this whole group
of men, is the sentence that keeps the platform safe:

> قُلْتُ: عَامَّةُ مَنْ سَعَى فِي دَمِ عُثْمَانَ قُتِلُوا، وَعَسَى القَتْلُ خَيْراً لَهُم وَتَمْحِيْصاً.
> — ⁨سیر أعلام النبلاء ج۳ ص۴۸۱⁩ · https://shamela.ws/book/10906/3089
> *English:* "I say: most of those who sought ⁨عثمان⁩'s blood were themselves killed — and perhaps
> that killing was better for them, and an expiation."

`[SOURCED]` ⁨سیر ج۳ ص۴۸۱⁩.

**One ⁨واقعہ⁩ worth telling — the last hour in Egypt.** ⁨قيس بن سعد ؓ⁩, whom he had come to replace, had
warned him before he ever took up the post: *nephew, be careful of these people — they will hand you
over and you will be killed.* ⁨الذهبي⁩ adds four words: `⁨فَكَانَ كَمَا قَالَ⁩` — and so it was
`[SOURCED]` ⁨سیر ج۳ ص۱۰۸⁩. His force was routed; he hid in the house of an Egyptian woman and she gave
him up — another report says it was an old woman and her own son who informed on him. Brought before
**⁨معاوية بن حديج⁩**, he said:

> احْفَظُونِي فِي أَبِي بَكْرٍ.
> — ⁨سیر أعلام النبلاء ج۳ ص۴۸۲⁩ · https://shamela.ws/book/10906/3090
> *English:* "Guard me for the sake of ⁨أبو بكر⁩."

And the answer:

> قَتَلْتَ ثَمَانِيْنَ مِنْ قَوْمِي فِي دَمِ الشَّهِيْدِ عُثْمَانَ، وَأَتْرُكُكَ، وَأَنْتَ صَاحِبُهُ!
> — ⁨سیر أعلام النبلاء ج۳ ص۴۸۲⁩ · https://shamela.ws/book/10906/3090
> *English:* "You killed eighty of my people over the blood of the martyr ⁨عثمان⁩ — and shall I let
> *you* go, who were his companion in it?"

He was killed, put inside the belly of a dead donkey, and burned `[SOURCED]` ⁨سیر ج۳ ص۴۸۲⁩ ·
سیر ج۳ ص۱۱۰.

**The date and manner of death, with the disagreement.** Two accounts of who killed him stand side by
side on the same page: **⁨معاوية بن حديج⁩** killed him (⁨الذهبي⁩'s main narration), and
**⁨عمرو بن دينار⁩** says he was brought captive to **⁨عمرو بن العاص ؓ⁩**, who killed him — *"meaning, for
⁨عثمان⁩"* `[SOURCED]` ⁨سیر ج۳ ص۴۸۲⁩. ⁨الذهبي⁩ records both and chooses neither.

**⚠ What not to say about him from the platform.** Do not narrate the burning. Do not narrate the
siege of ⁨عثمان ؓ⁩ through him. Do not set the son of ⁨الصديق ؓ⁩ against the daughter of ⁨الصديق ؓ⁩ — the
kinship is stated, and the evening moves on. If asked, the answer is the fiqh answer installed in
session 1: this is ⁨تاریخ⁩, not a ruling and not a creed, and we do not judge between the Companions.

---

## 6. هاشم بن عتبة ؓ

**The ⁨تراجم⁩ notice, in one breath**

- `name + ⁨لقب⁩` — **⁨هاشم بن عتبة بن أبي وقاص الزهري⁩**, known as **⁨المِرقال⁩** `[SOURCED]` ⁨سیر ج۳ ص۴۸۶⁩.
  The printed edition's editor, quoting ⁨الدولابي⁩ through ⁨الإصابة⁩, explains the ⁨لقب⁩: he was called
  ⁨المِرقال⁩ **because he used to press forward at speed in battle** — from ⁨الإرقال⁩, a kind of running
  `[SOURCED]` ⁨سیر ج۳ ص۴۸۶⁩, footnote (١).
- `dates` — **born in the Prophet's ﷺ lifetime**; **killed at ⁨صفين⁩** (37 AH) carrying ⁨علي ؓ⁩'s banner
  `[SOURCED]` ⁨سیر ج۳ ص۴۸۶⁩. On the Line: a single point, ⁨صفين⁩.
- `what he did` — he lost an eye at **⁨اليرموك⁩**, was present at the conquest of **Damascus**, and
  carried **⁨علي ؓ⁩'s own standard at ⁨صفين⁩**, where he was killed `[SOURCED]` ⁨سیر ج۳ ص۴۸۶⁩.

**Clan and kin.** **⁨بنو زهرة⁩** of Quraysh — the Prophet's ﷺ mother's clan. He is the **nephew of
⁨سعد بن أبي وقاص ؓ⁩**, the commander of ⁨القادسية⁩ and one of the six of the ⁨شورى⁩ `[SOURCED]` ⁨سیر⁩
⁨ج۳ ص۴۸۶⁩. That is the fact that makes him legible to the room in one breath: *⁨سعد ؓ⁩ withdrew from the
fitna; ⁨سعد ؓ⁩'s nephew carried ⁨علي ؓ⁩'s banner and died under it.*

**A point of precision the note must not blur.** ⁨الذهبي⁩ does **not** flatly call him a Companion:

> وَبَعْضُهُم عَدَّهُ فِي الصَّحَابَةِ، بِاعْتِبَارِ إِدْرَاكِ زَمَنِ النُّبُوَّةِ.
> — ⁨سیر أعلام النبلاء ج۳ ص۴۸۶⁩ · https://shamela.ws/book/10906/3094
> *English:* "Some have counted him among the Companions, on the ground that he lived in the time of
> prophethood."

`[SOURCED]` ⁨سیر ج۳ ص۴۸۶⁩. ⁨الذهبي⁩'s own closing formula for him on this page is **⁨رحمہ اللہ⁩**, not ؓ.
The safest usage on a slide: name him, give the ⁨لقب⁩, and use **⁨رحمہ اللہ⁩** as ⁨الذهبي⁩ does; if the ؓ
is used, say that some counted him a Companion because he lived in the Prophet's ﷺ time.

**Before 23 AH.** He was born while the Prophet ﷺ was alive, fought at **⁨اليرموك⁩** and lost an eye
there, and was at the **conquest of Damascus** `[SOURCED]` ⁨سیر ج۳ ص۴۸۶⁩. That is everything this page
gives, and nothing is added to it.

**In this window.** ⁨الذهبي⁩'s whole account of his role:

> مِنْ أُمَرَاءِ عَلِيٍّ يَوْمَ صِفِّيْنَ ... وَكَانَ مَعَهُ رَايَةُ الإِمَامِ عَلِيٍّ يَوْمَ صِفِّيْنَ، فَقُتِلَ يَوْمَئِذٍ (٢) . وَكَانَ مَوْصُوَفاً بِالشَّجَاعَةِ وَالإِقدَامِ - رَحِمَهُ اللهُ تَعَالَى -.
> — ⁨سیر أعلام النبلاء ج۳ ص۴۸۶⁩ · https://shamela.ws/book/10906/3094
> *English:* "One of ⁨علي⁩'s commanders on the day of ⁨صفين⁩ ... the standard of the Imām ⁨علي⁩ was with
> him on the day of ⁨صفين⁩, and he was killed that day. He was described as a man of courage and of
> pressing forward — may God have mercy on him."

`[SOURCED]` ⁨سیر ج۳ ص۴۸۶⁩.

**His own words.** None on this page. **The statement is: none in the sources we use.**

**The date and manner of death.** Killed at **⁨صفين⁩**, carrying the standard. The editor traces the
report to ⁨يعقوب بن شيبة⁩ from ⁨حبيب بن أبي ثابت⁩ and to ⁨يعقوب بن سفيان⁩ from ⁨الزهري⁩, through ⁨الإصابة⁩
۳/۵۹۳ `[SOURCED]` ⁨سیر ج۳ ص۴۸۶⁩, footnote (٢). No day, no month is given on this page — do not supply
one.

---

## 7. حجر بن عدي

**The ⁨تراجم⁩ notice, in one breath**

- `name + ⁨لقب⁩` — **⁨حجر بن عدي بن جبلة الكندي⁩**, ⁨أبو عبد الرحمن⁩ — ⁨الذهبي⁩ calls him **⁨حُجْر الخير⁩**
  (to separate him from his cousin, below) and **⁨الشهيد⁩** `[SOURCED]` ⁨سیر ج۳ ص۴۶۲⁩–۴۶۳.
- `dates` — `⁨لَهُ⁩: ⁨صُحْبَةٌ، وَوِفَادَةٌ⁩` — he has companionship and a delegation-visit, but **no
  narration from the Prophet ﷺ**; ⁨ابن سعد⁩ calls him `⁨جَاهِلِيّاً، إِسْلاَمِيّاً⁩`. **Killed at
  ⁨مرج عذراء⁩ outside Damascus in 51 AH** `[SOURCED]` ⁨سیر ج۳ ص۴۶۳⁩ · ⁨ج۳ ص۴۶۷⁩. On the Line: from the
  delegations to the eleventh year of Muʿāwiya's caliphate — **ten years past this session's window,
  and that is the point: he is the consequence.**
- `what he did` — he fought at ⁨القادسية⁩, opened **⁨مرج عذراء⁩** (the very place he was later executed),
  commanded at ⁨صفين⁩, refused to stop objecting publicly in Kufa, and was executed with his companions
  `[SOURCED]` ⁨سیر ج۳ ص۴۶۳⁩–۴۶۷.

**Clan and kin.** **⁨كندة⁩** — the same great Yemeni tribe as **⁨الأشعث بن قيس ؓ⁩**, and the two names sit
in the same genealogy. ⁨الذهبي⁩ heads ⁨حجر⁩:

> حُجْرُ بنُ عَدِيِّ بنِ جَبَلَةَ بنِ عَدِيِّ بنِ رَبِيْعَةَ الكِنْدِيُّ ** ابْنِ مُعَاوِيَةَ الأَكْرَمِيْنَ بنِ الحَارِثِ بنِ مُعَاوِيَةَ
> — ⁨سیر أعلام النبلاء ج۳ ص۴۶۲⁩ · https://shamela.ws/book/10906/3070
> *English:* "⁨حجر بن عدي بن جبلة بن عدي بن ربيعة الكندي⁩, son of ⁨معاوية الأكرمين بن الحارث بن معاوية⁩."

and heads ⁨الأشعث⁩:

> الأَشْعَثُ بنُ قَيْسِ بنِ مَعْدِيْ كَرِبَ الكِنْدِيُّ * (ع) ابْنِ مُعَاوِيَةَ بنِ جَبَلَةَ بنِ عَدِيِّ بنِ رَبِيْعَةَ بنِ مُعَاوِيَةَ الأَكْرَمِيْنَ بنِ الحَارِثِ بنِ مُعَاوِيَةَ بنِ ثَوْرِ بنِ مُرْتِعِ بنِ كِنْدَةَ.
> — ⁨سیر أعلام النبلاء ج۲ ص۳۷⁩ · https://shamela.ws/book/10906/2017
> *English:* "⁨الأشعث بن قيس بن معدي كرب الكندي⁩, son of ⁨معاوية بن جبلة بن عدي بن ربيعة بن معاوية الأكرمين بن الحارث بن معاوية بن ثور بن مرتع بن كندة⁩."

The two lines meet at ⁨جبلة بن عدي بن ربيعة⁩ `[SOURCED]` ⁨سیر ج۳ ص۴۶۲⁩ · ⁨ج۲ ص۳۷⁩. His father is **⁨عدي الأدبر⁩**, so called because he was speared while turning away;
his brother is **⁨هانئ بن الأدبر⁩**, with whom he came on the delegation `[SOURCED]` ⁨سیر ج۳ ص۴۶۳⁩. He
left two sons, **⁨عبيد الله⁩** and **⁨عبد الرحمن⁩**, both killed by **⁨مصعب بن الزبير⁩** `[SOURCED]` ⁨سیر⁩
ج۳ ص۴۶۷.

**Before 23 AH.** He came with his brother in a delegation to the Prophet ﷺ, and **narrated nothing
from him**; he heard from **⁨علي ؓ⁩** and **⁨عمار ؓ⁩**; he fought at **⁨القادسية⁩** `[SOURCED]` ⁨سیر ج۳ ص۴۶۳⁩.

**In this window and after it.** ⁨الذهبي⁩'s own description:

> وَكَانَ شَرِيفاً، أَمِيْراً مُطَاعاً، أَمَّاراً بِالمَعْرُوفِ، مُقْدِماً عَلَى الإِنْكَارِ، مِنْ شِيْعَةِ عَلِيٍّ -رَضِيَ اللهُ عَنْهُمَا -. شَهِدَ صِفِّيْنَ أَمِيْراً، وَكَانَ ذَا صَلاَحٍ وَتَعَبُّدٍ.
> — ⁨سیر أعلام النبلاء ج۳ ص۴۶۳⁩ · https://shamela.ws/book/10906/3071
> *English:* "He was a man of standing, a commander who was obeyed, much given to enjoining the good
> and forward in objecting; of ⁨علي⁩'s party — may God be pleased with them both. He was present at
> ⁨صفين⁩ as a commander, and he was a man of piety and devotion."

`[SOURCED]` ⁨سیر ج۳ ص۴۶۳⁩. Afterwards, in Kufa under **⁨زياد⁩**, he contradicted the governor from the
floor of the mosque while he was preaching, and on another occasion pelted him; ⁨زياد⁩ wrote to
⁨معاوية ؓ⁩ about him; he mustered three thousand armed men and left Kufa, then thought better of it and
sat down; ⁨زياد⁩, fearing a second rising, sent him under guard to Damascus with a group of his men
`[SOURCED]` ⁨سیر ج۳ ص۴۶۳⁩. ⁨زياد⁩'s first warning to him is a whole scene in itself, and ends
`⁨فَاكْفِنِي نَفْسَكَ⁩` — *spare me the trouble of you* `[SOURCED]` ⁨سیر ج۳ ص۴۶۳⁩–۴۶۴.

**One ⁨واقعہ⁩ worth telling — the two rakʿas at ⁨عذراء⁩.** When he was brought to the village he asked
its name; they told him **⁨عذراء⁩**; and he said: *by God, I am the first Muslim who ever set its dogs
barking in the way of God* — the place he had himself opened, thirty years before `[SOURCED]` ⁨سیر⁩
⁨ج۳ ص۴۶۴⁩–۴۶۵. Then he asked to pray two rakʿas, and made them long.

> فَقِيْلَ لَهُ: طَوَّلْتَ، أَجَزِعْتَ؟ فَقَالَ: مَا صَلَّيْتُ صَلاَةً أَخَفَّ مِنْهَا، وَلَئِنْ جَزِعْتُ لَقَدْ رَأَيْتُ سَيْفاً مَشْهُوْراً، وَكَفَناً مَنْشُوراً، وَقَبْراً مَحْفُوراً.
> — ⁨سیر أعلام النبلاء ج۳ ص۴۶۵⁩ · https://shamela.ws/book/10906/3073
> *English:* "He was told: you made it long — have you lost your nerve? He said: I have never prayed
> a lighter prayer than that one. And if I had lost my nerve — I have just seen a drawn sword, an
> unrolled shroud and a dug grave."

`[SOURCED]` ⁨سیر ج۳ ص۴۶۵⁩. Their own clansmen had brought the shrouds and dug the graves; and it is
also said it was ⁨معاوية ؓ⁩ who had that done `[SOURCED]` ⁨سیر ج۳ ص۴۶۵⁩.

**His own words.**

> اللَّهُمَّ إِنَّا نَسْتَعْدِيكَ (٣) عَلَى أُمَّتِنَا، فَإِنَّ أَهْلَ العِرَاقِ شَهِدُوا عَلَيْنَا، وَإِنَّ أَهْلَ الشَّامِ قَتَلُوْنَا.
> — ⁨سیر أعلام النبلاء ج۳ ص۴۶۵⁩ · https://shamela.ws/book/10906/3073
> *English:* "O God, we call upon You against our own community: the people of Iraq testified against
> us, and the people of Syria killed us."

> فَقِيْلَ لَهُ: مُدَّ عُنُقَكَ. فَقَالَ: إِنَّ ذَلِكَ لَدَمٌ مَا كُنْتُ لأُعِيْنَ عَلَيْهِ.
> — ⁨سیر أعلام النبلاء ج۳ ص۴۶۵⁩ · https://shamela.ws/book/10906/3073
> *English:* "He was told: stretch out your neck. He said: that is blood — I am not going to help in
> shedding it."

> ادْفِنُوْنِي فِي ثِيَابِي، فَإِنِّيْ أُبْعَثُ مُخَاصِماً.
> — ⁨سیر أعلام النبلاء ج۳ ص۴۶۶⁩ · https://shamela.ws/book/10906/3074
> *English:* "Bury me in my clothes, for I shall be raised up as a litigant." (⁨ابن سيرين⁩, through
> ابن عون.)

**The date and manner of death, with the disagreements.** **51 AH**, at **⁨عذراء⁩**; their burial place
is still visible there and is visited `[SOURCED]` ⁨سیر ج۳ ص۴۶۷⁩. The numbers: they were **thirteen**;
**seven** were killed and **six** survived, because ⁨معاوية ؓ⁩'s messenger with the release arrived
after the seventh `[SOURCED]` ⁨سیر ج۳ ص۴۶۵⁩. It is also said the executioner was **⁨هدبة بن فياض⁩**, a
one-eyed man, and that one of the Khathʿam men read the omen: *if the bird told true, half of us will
be killed and half will escape* `[SOURCED]` ⁨سیر ج۳ ص۴۶۵⁩. A further report, introduced with `⁨وَقِيْلَ⁩`,
has ⁨معاوية ؓ⁩'s envoy offering them disavowal and repentance, ten refusing and ten accepting
`[SOURCED]` ⁨سیر ج۳ ص۴۶۶⁩ — the arithmetic does not match the thirteen, and ⁨الذهبي⁩ does not reconcile
it. **Record the mismatch; do not smooth it.**

**What the sources record about the reaction.** ⁨عائشہ ؓ⁩ sent **⁨عبد الرحمن بن الحارث بن هشام⁩** to
⁨معاوية ؓ⁩ to ask that they be released; the messenger arrived after they were killed, and said:
*Commander of the Faithful, where did the forbearance of ⁨أبو سفيان⁩ go from you?* — and ⁨معاوية ؓ⁩
answered, *with the absence of men like you from me*, `⁨يَعْنِي⁩: ⁨أَنَّهُ نَدِمَ⁩`, **meaning that he
regretted it** `[SOURCED]` ⁨سیر ج۳ ص۴۶۴⁩ · ⁨ج۳ ص۴۶۵⁩. **⁨ابن عمر ؓ⁩** was in the market when the news of
⁨حجر⁩ reached him; he loosened his wrap, stood up, and was overcome with weeping — the editor traces it
to ⁨أحمد⁩ through ⁨ابن علية⁩ and calls it **⁨صحيح⁩** `[SOURCED]` ⁨سیر ج۳ ص۴۶۶⁩ and its footnote (٥). And when
⁨معاوية ؓ⁩ asked leave to enter upon ⁨عائشہ ؓ⁩:

> أَقَتَلْتَ
> — ⁨سیر أعلام النبلاء ج۳ ص۴۶۶⁩ · https://shamela.ws/book/10906/3074
> حُجْراً؟ قَالَ: وَجَدْتُ فِي قَتْلِهِ صَلاَحَ النَّاسِ، وَخِفْتُ مِنْ فَسَادِهِم (١) .
> — ⁨سیر أعلام النبلاء ج۳ ص۴۶۷⁩ · https://shamela.ws/book/10906/3075
> *English:* "'Did you kill ⁨حجر⁩?' He said: 'I found in his killing the wellbeing of the people, and
> I feared their corruption.'"
>
> *Flag:* the quotation straddles the page break — `⁨أَقَتَلْتَ⁩` is the last word of ⁨ص۴۶۶⁩
> (index 3074); everything from `⁨حُجْراً؟⁩` onward is on ⁨ص۴۶۷⁩ (index 3075).

`[SOURCED]` ⁨سیر ج۳ ص۴۶۶⁩–۴۶۷; the editor traces it to ⁨أحمد⁩ through ⁨ابن علية⁩ from ⁨أيوب⁩, from
⁨عبد الله بن أبي مليكة⁩ **or someone else** — note the uncertainty in the chain, which is in the
footnote itself.

**The other ⁨حجر⁩ — a necessary disambiguation.** **⁨حجر الشر⁩, ⁨حجر بن يزيد بن سلمة الكندي⁩**, is his
**paternal cousin**, a different man: he came in a delegation to the Prophet ﷺ, was then of ⁨علي ؓ⁩'s
party, **was present at the day of the two arbitrators**, and afterwards became one of ⁨معاوية ؓ⁩'s
governors, over **⁨أرمينية⁩**. He too narrates nothing `[SOURCED]` ⁨سیر ج۳ ص۴۶۷⁩. Two cousins of one
clan, one executed by the state and one governing for it — and the room will confuse them unless the
speaker separates them by name.

**⚠ What not to say about him from the platform.** This is the single most combustible ⁨ترجمه⁩ in the
note. Narrate what ⁨الذهبي⁩ narrates — the objection, the letter, the sending, the execution, the
regret, ⁨عائشہ ؓ⁩'s intercession, ⁨ابن عمر ؓ⁩'s tears, ⁨معاوية ؓ⁩'s own stated reason — **and stop.** Do not
pronounce on whether the execution was lawful. Do not connect it forward to ⁨كربلا⁩. Do not let the
word ⁨شيعة⁩, which ⁨الذهبي⁩ uses here as a plain descriptor (`⁨مِنْ شِيْعَةِ عَلِيٍّ⁩`), be heard as the
later sect; say "of ⁨علي ؓ⁩'s party" and move on.

---

## 8. صعصعة بن صوحان

**The ⁨تراجم⁩ notice, in one breath**

- `name + ⁨لقب⁩` — **⁨صعصعة بن صوحان⁩, ⁨أبو طلحة⁩** — ⁨الذهبي⁩ heads him **⁨أَحَدُ خُطَبَاءِ العَرَبِ⁩**, one
  of the orators of the Arabs `[SOURCED]` ⁨سیر ج۳ ص۵۲۸⁩. ⁨الذهبي⁩ does **not** record ⁨صحبة⁩ for him; he is
  listed with the symbol (⁨س⁩) as a narrator and his teachers are **⁨علي ؓ⁩** and **⁨ابن عباس ؓ⁩**
  `[SOURCED]` ⁨سیر ج۳ ص۵۲۸⁩–۵۲۹. No ؓ is used for him here.
- `dates` — no birth date on the page `[STANDARD]` *(to verify)*; `⁨وَبَقِيَ إِلَى خِلاَفَةِ⁩
  ⁨مُعَاوِيَةَ⁩` — **he lived into ⁨معاوية ؓ⁩'s caliphate** `[SOURCED]` ⁨سیر ج۳ ص۵۲۹⁩. On the Line: from
  ⁨الجمل⁩ (36 AH) into the 40s.
- `what he did` — **he picked up the banner at ⁨الجمل⁩ when his two brothers had been killed under it**,
  and he was one of the senior men of ⁨علي ؓ⁩'s following `[SOURCED]` ⁨سیر ج۳ ص۵۲۸⁩–۵۲۹.

**Clan and kin.** ⁨عبد القيس⁩ of ⁨البصرة⁩ `[STANDARD]` *(to verify)* — this page gives the family, not the
tribe. What it does give is the family, and it is the point of the entry: his brothers **⁨زيد بن⁩
⁨صوحان⁩** and **⁨سيحان⁩** were both killed **on the day of ⁨الجمل⁩**, and ⁨صعصعة⁩ took the standard from them
`[SOURCED]` ⁨سیر ج۳ ص۵۲۸⁩–۵۲۹. Three brothers, one banner, one day.

**In this window.** ⁨الذهبي⁩'s whole notice:

> أَحَدُ خُطَبَاءِ العَرَبِ. كَانَ مِنْ كِبَارِ أَصْحَابِ عَلِيٍّ. قُتِلَ
> — ⁨سیر أعلام النبلاء ج۳ ص۵۲۸⁩ · https://shamela.ws/book/10906/3136
> أَخَوَاهُ يَوْمَ الجَمَلِ، فَأَخَذَ صَعْصَعَةُ الرَّايَةَ.
> — ⁨سیر أعلام النبلاء ج۳ ص۵۲۹⁩ · https://shamela.ws/book/10906/3137
> *English:* "One of the orators of the Arabs. He was among the senior of ⁨علي⁩'s companions. His two
> brothers were killed on the day of ⁨الجمل⁩, and ⁨صعصعة⁩ took up the standard."

> وَثَّقَهُ: ابْنُ سَعْدٍ. وَكَانَ شَرِيْفاً، مُطَاعاً، أَمِيْراً، فَصِيْحاً، مُفَوَّهاً.
> — ⁨سیر أعلام النبلاء ج۳ ص۵۲۹⁩ · https://shamela.ws/book/10906/3137
> *English:* "⁨ابن سعد⁩ declared him trustworthy. He was a man of standing, obeyed, a commander,
> eloquent, and a fine speaker."

`[SOURCED]` ⁨سیر ج۳ ص۵۲۸⁩–۵۲۹.

**One ⁨واقعہ⁩ worth telling — his brother's burial instructions.** ⁨زيد بن صوحان⁩, dying at ⁨الجمل⁩, gave
orders about his own body that the room will not forget:

> لاَ تَغْسِلُوا عَنِّي دَماً، وَلاَ تَنْزِعُوا عَنِّي ثَوْباً، إِلاَّ الخُفَّيْنِ، وَأَرْمِسُونِي فِي الأَرْضِ رَمْساً، فَإِنِّي مُخَاصِمٌ، أُحَاجُّ يَوْمَ القِيَامَةِ.
> — ⁨سیر أعلام النبلاء ج۳ ص۵۲۸⁩ · https://shamela.ws/book/10906/3136
> *English:* "Do not wash any blood off me, and do not take any garment off me except the two
> leather socks; and press me into the earth. For I am a litigant: I shall plead on the Day of
> Resurrection."

`[SOURCED]` ⁨سیر ج۳ ص۵۲۸⁩, through ⁨الثوري⁩ from ⁨مخول⁩ from ⁨العيزار بن حريث⁩ from ⁨زيد بن صوحان⁩; ⁨ابن سعد⁩
۶/۱۲۵. A second wording, through ⁨عمار الدهني⁩, has him say: *bury me and my mother's son in one grave,
and wash no blood off us, for we are a people who are litigants* `[SOURCED]` ⁨سیر ج۳ ص۵۲۸⁩ — and it is
said his brother ⁨سيحان⁩ was killed with him and the two were buried in one grave. **Note the exact
formula: it is word-for-word the formula ⁨حجر بن عدي⁩ used ten years later** (`⁨فَإِنِّيْ أُبْعَثُ⁩
⁨مُخَاصِماً⁩`, ⁨سیر ج۳ ص۴۶۶⁩). The repetition is the historical fact; the note does not explain it.

**His own words.** One exchange, and ⁨الذهبي⁩ introduces it with **⁨يُقَال⁩** — *it is said* — which the
speaker must reproduce:

> يُقَالُ: وَفَدَ عَلَى مُعَاوِيَةَ، فَخَطَبَ، فَقَالَ: إِنْ كُنْتُ لأُبْغِضُ أَنْ أَرَاكَ خَطِيْباً. قَالَ: وَأَنَا إِنْ كُنْتُ لأُبْغِضُ أَنْ أَرَاكَ خَلِيْفَةً.
> — ⁨سیر أعلام النبلاء ج۳ ص۵۲۹⁩ · https://shamela.ws/book/10906/3137
> *English:* "It is said that he came to ⁨معاوية⁩ and gave an oration, and ⁨معاوية⁩ said: 'I used to hate
> to see you an orator.' He said: 'And I used to hate to see you a caliph.'"

`[SOURCED]` ⁨سیر ج۳ ص۵۲۹⁩. ⁨الذهبي⁩ gives no chain for it and the editor refers it to ⁨ابن عساكر ۸⁩/۱۵۶,
where the whole oration is given.

**The date and manner of death.** **Not established on this page.** All ⁨الذهبي⁩ says is that he lived
into ⁨معاوية ؓ⁩'s caliphate `[SOURCED]` ⁨سیر ج۳ ص۵۲۹⁩. Do not give a year.

**⚠ What not to say about him from the platform.** The exchange with ⁨معاوية ؓ⁩ is sharp and the room
will enjoy it too much. If it is told at all it is told with ⁨الذهبي⁩'s own `⁨يُقَال⁩` in front of it and
no comment after it — and it is probably better told in the session that handles ⁨معاوية ؓ⁩'s caliphate
than in this one.

---

## 9. جرير بن عبد الله البجلي ؓ

**The ⁨تراجم⁩ notice, in one breath**

- `name + ⁨لقب⁩` — **⁨جرير بن عبد الله بن جابر البجلي القسري ؓ⁩**, ⁨أبو عمرو⁩ (and it is said ⁨أبو عبد الله⁩).
  ⁨الذهبي⁩ heads him **⁨الأَمِيْرُ، النَّبِيْلُ، الجَمِيْلُ⁩ ... ⁨مِنْ أَعْيَانِ الصَّحَابَةِ⁩**
  `[SOURCED]` ⁨سیر ج۲ ص۵۳۰⁩. His ⁨لقب⁩ is the one ⁨عمر ؓ⁩ gave him: **⁨يوسف هذه الأمة⁩** `[SOURCED]` ⁨سیر⁩
  ج۲ ص۵۳۵.
- `dates` — came to Medina, per ⁨الواقدي⁩, in **⁨رمضان⁩ of the year 10** with a hundred and fifty of his
  people — *among the last of the Companions to accept Islam*, as ⁨إبراهيم النخعي⁩ notes; died **51 AH**
  (⁨الهيثم⁩, ⁨خليفة⁩, ⁨محمد بن مثنى⁩) or **54** (⁨ابن الكلبي⁩) `[SOURCED]` ⁨سیر ج۲ ص۵۳۲⁩ · ⁨ج۲ ص۵۳۳⁩ · ⁨ج۲ ص۵۳۶⁩.
  On the Line: from the year of the delegations to the second decade of Muʿāwiya's caliphate.
- `what he did` — he destroyed **⁨ذو الخلصة⁩**, commanded the **right wing of ⁨سعد ؓ⁩ at ⁨القادسية⁩**,
  governed **⁨همذان⁩** for ⁨عثمان ؓ⁩, carried **⁨علي ؓ⁩'s demand for allegiance to ⁨معاوية ؓ⁩**, and then
  withdrew from both and stayed withdrawn until he died `[SOURCED]` ⁨سیر ج۲ ص۵۳۳⁩ · ⁨ج۲ ص۵۳۵⁩–۵۳۶.

**Clan and kin.** **⁨بجيلة⁩**, of the house of **⁨قَسْر⁩ — and ⁨قسر⁩ is of ⁨قحطان⁩**, that is, of the south
`[SOURCED]` ⁨سیر ج۲ ص۵۳۰⁩. He is therefore one of the Yemeni chiefs of ⁨الكوفة⁩, and that is why he
appears again and again as the man sent to reason with other Yemeni chiefs: he is one of the three
(with **⁨عدي بن حاتم⁩** and **⁨خالد بن عرفطة⁩**) whom ⁨زياد⁩ sent to **⁨حجر بن عدي⁩** to talk him down
`[SOURCED]` ⁨سیر ج۳ ص۴۶۴⁩; he is the man who stood up on the day **⁨المغيرة بن شعبة ؓ⁩** died and asked
the people to seek forgiveness for him `[SOURCED]` ⁨سیر ج۳ ص۳۱⁩; and it is he whom **⁨الأشعث بن قيس ؓ⁩**
pushed forward to lead a funeral prayer, with the reason given below `[SOURCED]` ⁨سیر ج۲ ص۴۰⁩.

**Before 23 AH.** The Prophet ﷺ announced him before he arrived:

> (إِنَّهُ سَيَدْخُلُ عَلَيْكُمْ مِنْ هَذَا الفَجِّ مِنْ خَيْرِ ذِي يَمَنٍ، أَلاَ وَإِنَّ عَلَى وَجْهِهِ مِسْحَةَ مَلَكٍ)
> — ⁨سیر أعلام النبلاء ج۲ ص۵۳۱⁩ · https://shamela.ws/book/10906/2511
> *English:* "There will come in upon you from this mountain pass one of the best of the men of
> Yemen — and on his face is the touch of an angel."

The editor marks this route `⁨إسناده قوي⁩`, and notes the first half is in ⁨البخاري⁩ and ⁨مسلم⁩
`[SOURCED]` ⁨سیر ج۲ ص۵۳۱⁩ and its footnotes. He was sent to destroy **⁨ذو الخلصة⁩**, "the Yemeni Kaʿba";
he told the Prophet ﷺ he could not sit a horse, and the Prophet ﷺ put his hand on his chest and said
`⁨اللَّهُمَّ اجْعَلْهُ هَادِياً مَهْدِيّاً⁩` — ⁨البخاري⁩ and ⁨مسلم⁩ both `[SOURCED]` ⁨سیر ج۲ ص۵۳۳⁩. He gave
the Prophet ﷺ his pledge **on sincere counsel to every Muslim** `[SOURCED]` ⁨سیر ج۲ ص۵۳۱⁩.

**In this window.** He was on **the right wing of ⁨سعد بن أبي وقاص ؓ⁩ on the day of ⁨القادسية⁩**
(⁨الشعبي⁩) `[SOURCED]` ⁨سیر ج۲ ص۵۳۵⁩. ⁨الهيثم بن عدي⁩: **his eye was lost at ⁨همذان⁩, when he governed it for
⁨عثمان ؓ⁩** `[SOURCED]` ⁨سیر ج۲ ص۵۳۶⁩. He settled in **⁨الكوفة⁩**, then in **⁨قرقيسياء⁩** on the Khābūr, and
came as **⁨علي ؓ⁩'s envoy to ⁨معاوية ؓ⁩** `[SOURCED]` ⁨سیر ج۲ ص۵۳۵⁩. And then he stepped out of the whole
thing. ⁨ابن سعد⁩, from ⁨الواقدي⁩:

> لَمْ يَزَلْ جَرِيْرٌ مُعْتَزِلاً لِعَلِيٍّ وَمُعَاوِيَةَ بِالجَزِيْرَةِ وَنَوَاحِيْهَا حَتَّى تُوُفِّيَ بِالشَّرَاةِ، فِي وِلايَةِ الضَّحَّاكِ بنِ قَيْسٍ عَلَى الكُوْفَةِ.
> — ⁨سیر أعلام النبلاء ج۲ ص۵۳۶⁩ · https://shamela.ws/book/10906/2516
> *English:* "⁨جرير⁩ never ceased to hold aloof from both ⁨علي⁩ and ⁨معاوية⁩, in ⁨الجزيرة⁩ and its
> districts, until he died at ⁨الشراة⁩, during ⁨الضحاك بن قيس⁩'s governorship of ⁨الكوفة⁩."

`[SOURCED]` ⁨سیر ج۲ ص۵۳۶⁩. The narrator is ⁨الواقدي⁩, whom the editor of this edition elsewhere calls
⁨متروك⁩ — **cite it as ⁨ابن سعد⁩'s report through ⁨الواقدي⁩, not as established fact.**

**One ⁨واقعہ⁩ worth telling — the reason he gave.** ⁨علي ؓ⁩ sent **⁨ابن عباس ؓ⁩** and **⁨الأشعث بن قيس ؓ⁩**
to him at ⁨قرقيسياء⁩ with a message: *the Commander of the Faithful greets you and says, what you did
in parting from ⁨معاوية⁩ was well seen, and I place you in the position the Messenger of God ﷺ placed
you in.* And ⁨جرير ؓ⁩ answered:

> إِنَّ رَسُوْلَ اللهِ -صَلَّى اللَّهُ عَلَيْهِ وَسَلَّمَ- بَعَثَنِي إِلَى اليَمَنِ، أُقَاتِلُهُمْ حَتَّى يَقُوْلُوا: لاَ إِلَهَ إِلاَّ اللهُ، فَإِذَا قَالُوا، حَرُمَتْ دِمَاؤُهُمْ وَأَمْوَالُهُمْ، فَلاَ أُقَاتِلُ مَنْ يَقُوْلُ: لاَ إِلَهَ إِلاَّ اللهُ.
> — ⁨سیر أعلام النبلاء ج۲ ص۵۳۶⁩ · https://shamela.ws/book/10906/2516
> *English:* "The Messenger of God ﷺ sent me to Yemen to fight them until they should say *there is
> no god but God*; and when they said it, their blood and their property became inviolable. So I do
> not fight a man who says *there is no god but God*."

**Grading, and it matters:** the editor notes that **⁨أبان بن عبد الله⁩ is weak in memory, and
⁨إبراهيم بن جرير⁩ did not hear from his father** `[SOURCED]` ⁨سیر ج۲ ص۵۳۶⁩, footnote (٣). The sentence is
superb and the room will remember it for ten years; it must therefore be delivered as *"this is how
⁨سیر⁩ records ⁨جرير ؓ⁩'s reason — and the chain is criticised"*, or not at all. Put the grading in the
speaker notes and the honest hedge in the mouth.

**His own words, and ⁨عمر ؓ⁩ on him.**

> فَقَالَ عُمَرُ: عَزَمْتُ عَلَى صَاحِبِ هَذِهِ لَمَا قَامَ فَتَوَضَّأَ. فَقَالَ جَرِيْرٌ: اعْزِمْ عَلَيْنَا جَمِيْعاً. فَقَالَ: عَزَمْتُ عَلَيَّ وَعَلَيْكُمْ لَمَا قُمْنَا، فَتَوَضَّأْنَا، ثُمَّ صَلَّيْنَا.
> — ⁨سیر أعلام النبلاء ج۲ ص۵۳۵⁩ · https://shamela.ws/book/10906/2515
> *English:* "⁨عمر⁩ said: I require the man responsible for that to get up and make wuḍūʾ. ⁨جرير⁩ said:
> require it of all of us. So he said: I require it of myself and of you all — that we rise, make
> wuḍūʾ, and then pray."

Some narrators add that ⁨عمر ؓ⁩ then said to him: *may God have mercy on you — what a chief you were in
the Jāhiliyya, and what a chief you have been in Islam* `[SOURCED]` ⁨سیر ج۲ ص۵۳۵⁩.

> جَرِيْرٌ يُوْسُفُ هَذِهِ الأُمَّةِ.
> — ⁨سیر أعلام النبلاء ج۲ ص۵۳۵⁩ · https://shamela.ws/book/10906/2515
> *English:* "⁨جرير⁩ is the Joseph of this Umma." (⁨عمر ؓ⁩, through ⁨إبراهيم بن جرير⁩; the editor:
> `رجاله ثقات`.)

**The date and manner of death.** **51 AH** per ⁨الهيثم بن عدي⁩, ⁨خليفة⁩ and ⁨محمد بن مثنى⁩; **54** per
⁨ابن الكلبي⁩; at **⁨الشراة⁩**, per ⁨ابن سعد⁩'s report `[SOURCED]` ⁨سیر ج۲ ص۵۳۶⁩. His musnad is about a
hundred ḥadīth with repetitions; the two Shaykhs agree on eight `[SOURCED]` ⁨سیر ج۲ ص۵۳۷⁩.

**⚠ What not to say about him from the platform.** The report describing what he found when he
reached ⁨معاوية ؓ⁩ — the people weeping round ⁨عثمان ؓ⁩'s shirt hung on a spear — is carried by
**⁨عمران بن عبد العزيز⁩**, of whom **⁨يحيى القطان⁩ and ⁨البخاري⁩ both say ⁨منكر الحديث⁩**, as the editor notes
on the page `[SOURCED]` ⁨سیر ج۲ ص۵۳۶⁩ and footnote (١). It is a vivid image and it is exactly the kind
of thing that ends up on a slide. **It must not.**

---

## 10. الأشعث بن قيس ؓ

**The ⁨تراجم⁩ notice, in one breath**

- `name + ⁨لقب⁩` — **⁨الأشعث بن قيس بن معدي كرب الكندي ؓ⁩**. **His real name was ⁨معدي كرب⁩**; he was
  perpetually tousle-headed, and *⁨الأشعث⁩* — "the dishevelled" — stuck `[SOURCED]` ⁨سیر ج۲ ص۳۸⁩. ⁨الذهبي⁩
  gives him no honorific ⁨لقب⁩, and none is invented here.
- `dates` — came in a delegation of **seventy of ⁨كندة⁩** (⁨ابن الكلبي⁩); **died in 40 AH, forty nights
  after ⁨علي ؓ⁩**, aged sixty-three by one report `[SOURCED]` ⁨سیر ج۲ ص۳۸⁩ · ⁨ج۲ ص۴۲⁩. On the Line: from the
  delegations to forty nights past the end of the Rāshidūn window — he dies inside this session.
- `what he did` — he apostatised in the ⁨ردة⁩ and was brought captive to ⁨أبو بكر ؓ⁩, who freed him and
  married his own sister to him; he lost an eye at **⁨اليرموك⁩**; he governed **⁨أذربيجان⁩** for ⁨عثمان ؓ⁩;
  and `⁨كَانَ أَكْبَرَ أُمَرَاءِ عَلِيٍّ يَوْمَ صِفِّيْنَ⁩` — **he was the most senior of ⁨علي ؓ⁩'s
  commanders on the day of ⁨صفين⁩** `[SOURCED]` ⁨سیر ج۲ ص۳۸⁩ · ⁨ج۲ ص۳۹⁩ · ⁨ج۲ ص۴۱⁩.

**Clan and kin.** **⁨كندة⁩** — the same tribe as **⁨حجر بن عدي⁩**, and the two genealogies converge at
⁨جبلة بن عدي بن ربيعة⁩ — see the two headings quoted in §7 `[SOURCED]` ⁨سیر ج۲ ص۳۷⁩ · ⁨ج۳ ص۴۶۲⁩. He is the **brother-in-law
of ⁨أبو بكر الصديق ؓ⁩**: ⁨أبو بكر ؓ⁩ married him to **⁨فروة بنت أبي قحافة⁩**, his own sister `[SOURCED]`
⁨سیر ج۲ ص۳۹⁩. **His daughter was the wife of ⁨الحسن بن علي ؓ⁩**, which is why ⁨الحسن ؓ⁩ came to his funeral
and prayed over him `[SOURCED]` ⁨سیر ج۲ ص۴۲⁩. His son **⁨محمد بن الأشعث⁩** became one of the great
commanders; his grandson **⁨عبد الرحمن بن محمد بن الأشعث⁩** led the rising against **⁨الحجاج⁩**, in which,
it is said, **eighty-one pitched battles** were fought, most of them going against ⁨الحجاج⁩, before
⁨ابن الأشعث⁩ was abandoned, routed and destroyed `[SOURCED]` ⁨سیر ج۲ ص۴۲⁩–۴۳. **One family: the ⁨ردة⁩, ⁨صفين⁩
and the war with ⁨الحجاج⁩, in three generations.**

**Before 23 AH.** A verse of ⁨آل عمران⁩ was revealed about a case of his before the Prophet ﷺ, and he
said so himself — ⁨البخاري⁩ and ⁨مسلم⁩ both carry it `[SOURCED]` ⁨سیر ج۲ ص۳۸⁩. Then the ⁨ردة⁩:
⁨إبراهيم النخعي⁩ records that ⁨الأشعث⁩ apostatised with a number of ⁨كندة⁩, was besieged, and took safe
conduct — **for seventy men, and not for himself**. He was brought to ⁨الصديق ؓ⁩, who said: *we are
going to kill you; you have no safe conduct.* He said: *will you grant me favour, and I become
Muslim?* And ⁨أبو بكر ؓ⁩ did, and married his sister to him `[SOURCED]` ⁨سیر ج۲ ص۳۹⁩.

**One ⁨واقعہ⁩ worth telling — the wedding feast.** ⁨قيس بن أبي حازم⁩ tells it: when ⁨الأشعث⁩ was brought
captive to ⁨أبو بكر ؓ⁩, ⁨أبو بكر ؓ⁩ loosed his bonds and married his sister to him. So ⁨الأشعث⁩ drew his
sword, walked into the camel market of Medina, and began hamstringing every camel and every bull he
saw. The people shouted: *⁨الأشعث⁩ has apostatised!* Then he threw the sword down and said:

> وَاللهِ مَا كَفَرْتُ؛ وَلَكِنَّ هَذَا الرَّجُلَ زَوَّجَنِي أُخْتَهُ؛ وَلَو كُنَّا فِي بِلاَدِنَا لَكَانَتْ لَنَا وَلِيْمَةٌ غَيْرُ هَذِهِ، يَا أَهْلَ المَدِيْنَةِ، انْحَرُوا، وَكُلُوا! وَيَا أَهْلَ الإِبِلِ تَعَالَوْا خُذُوا شَرْوَاهَا!
> — ⁨سیر أعلام النبلاء ج۲ ص۳۹⁩ · https://shamela.ws/book/10906/2019
> *English:* "By God, I have not disbelieved. This man has married his sister to me, and had we been
> in our own country we would have had a wedding feast other than this one. People of Medina —
> slaughter and eat! And owners of the camels, come and take the like of them!"

`[SOURCED]` ⁨سیر ج۲ ص۳۹⁩; the editor: ⁨الطبراني⁩ (۶۴۹), `⁨إسناده صحيح ورجاله رجال الصحيح⁩` except
⁨عبد المؤمن بن علي⁩, who is trustworthy `[SOURCED]` ⁨سیر ج۲ ص۳۹⁩, footnote (٣).

**And the sentence he said about himself.** At a funeral, with ⁨جرير ؓ⁩ present, ⁨الأشعث ؓ⁩ put ⁨جرير ؓ⁩
forward to lead, and gave his reason:

> إِنَّ هَذَا لَمْ يَرْتَدَّ، وَإِنِّي ارْتَدَدْتُ.
> — ⁨سیر أعلام النبلاء ج۲ ص۴۰⁩ · https://shamela.ws/book/10906/2020
> *English:* "This man did not apostatise. And I did."

`[SOURCED]` ⁨سیر ج۲ ص۴۰⁩ (through ⁨إسماعيل⁩ from ⁨قيس⁩; ⁨الحافظ⁩ traces it to ⁨ابن السكن⁩ and others). This is
the card that saves the whole figure from caricature.

**In this window.** He was **⁨عثمان ؓ⁩'s governor of ⁨أذربيجان⁩** — once he expiated an oath at a cost of
**fifteen thousand**, and on another occasion, having sworn truly, gave back **thirty thousand**
rather than keep it, saying *"God disfigure you for a piece of property! By God, I only swore over
what was true — but this is a return to its owner"* `[SOURCED]` ⁨سیر ج۲ ص۴۱⁩. At **⁨صفين⁩** he commanded
**⁨علي ؓ⁩'s right wing** (⁨أبو عبيدة⁩), and when ⁨معاوية ؓ⁩'s men took the Euphrates first, ⁨علي ؓ⁩ sent
⁨الأشعث⁩ with **two thousand** against **⁨أبو الأعور⁩ with five thousand**, and ⁨الأشعث⁩ took the water
`[SOURCED]` ⁨سیر ج۲ ص۴۰⁩. When he was reproached for having gone out with ⁨علي ؓ⁩ after all his warnings
about the fitan, he answered: `⁨وَمَنْ لَكَ إِمَامٌ مِثْلُ عَلِيٍّ⁩` — *and where will you find an imām
like ⁨علي⁩?* — ⁨البخاري⁩ carries it in ⁨التاريخ الكبير⁩ `[SOURCED]` ⁨سیر ج۲ ص۴۰⁩. He also, at least once,
stood up to ⁨علي ؓ⁩ to his face, and ⁨علي ؓ⁩ answered him: *do you threaten me with death? I do not care
for it — bring me a collar and a fetter!* — and his companions interceded and ⁨علي ؓ⁩ let him be
`[SOURCED]` ⁨سیر ج۲ ص۴۰⁩–۴۱.

**His own words — the appeal over the water at ⁨صفين⁩.** ⁨أبو الصلت الحضرمي⁩, a Syrian, tells it from the
other side of the river: *we had cut the people of Iraq off from the water. A horseman came up and
uncovered his face, and it was ⁨الأشعث بن قيس⁩.*
